### [1] [Preserving Item Semantics for Free: Rethinking Token Initialization in LLM-Based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07816)
*Donald Loveland,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Danai Koutra,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的质心初始化方法，将语义ID（SID）的token嵌入直接初始化为其在语义嵌入空间中的质心，而非随机高斯向量，从而保留SID的连续几何结构。该参数免费、无需额外训练开销的方法在纯SFT下将Recall@5提升最多16%，冷物品Recall@5提升最多60%，并能在40%更少的SFT步骤中达到峰值性能；在需要额外CPT的数据集上，质心初始化仅用一半CPT轮数即可达到相当性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于大语言模型的生成式推荐中，物品通过语义ID（SID）加入LLM词表，但标准词表扩展使用随机高斯向量初始化token，丢弃了SID底层的连续几何结构，迫使LLM从交互数据中重新学习token关系。作者首先证明这种初始化导致SID嵌入围绕物品流行度而非语义组织，且昂贵的持续预训练（CPT）无法可靠恢复原始语义几何。

Method: 提出一种参数免费的介入方法：将SID token嵌入直接初始化为其在语义嵌入空间中的对应质心（centroid）。该方法只需几行代码，不增加训练或推理开销，可作为drop-in替换方案应用到现有GR系统中。

Result: 质心初始化在纯SFT下提升Recall@5最多16%，以最多40%更少的SFT步数达到峰值性能，冷物品Recall@5提升最多60%；在需要额外CPT的数据集上，以一半CPT轮数达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 保留SID几何结构（超越共享前缀结构）为基于LLM的生成式推荐提供了一种简单有效的语义先验，证明了初始化方式对GR系统性能的关键影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative recommendation (GR) leverage large language models (LLMs) as recommender backbones, enabling LLMs to directly generate recommendations conditioned on item-interaction histories. In these systems, items are often represented through semantic IDs (SIDs) added to the LLM vocabulary as special tokens. Ideally, SIDs imbue item token representations with semantic priors, thereby improving model generalization. However, standard vocabulary expansion typically initializes these tokens as random Gaussian vectors, discarding the SIDs' underlying continuous geometry and forcing the LLM to relearn token relationships from interaction data. To demonstrate the consequences of this design, we first show that training from this initialization tends to organize SID embeddings around item popularity rather than semantics. We further show that, despite partially reducing the reliance on popularity and improving cold item performance, the computationally expensive process of continual pretraining (CPT) fails to reliably recover the original semantic geometry. To address these findings, we propose a simple, parameter-free intervention that initializes SID token embeddings directly from their corresponding centroids in the semantic embedding space. Requiring only a few lines of code and no additional training or inference overhead, this drop-in approach improves pure-SFT Recall@5 by up to 16%, reaches peak performance with up to 40% fewer SFT steps, and improves cold-item Recall@5 by up to 60%. Moreover, on datasets that benefit from additional CPT, centroid initialization reaches comparable performance while requiring half as many CPT epochs. Together, our findings show that preserving SID geometry, beyond shared-prefix structure, provides a simple and effective semantic prior for LLM-based GR.

</details>


### [2] [PushDualGen: Enabling LLMs to Generate Semantic IDs with Interpretable Copy for Industrial Push Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07989)
*Manjia Lin,Da Li,Yan Wang,Yong Jin,Zheming Ding,Wei Yuan,Lei Yan,Yanan Xia,Lu Zhang,Fan Yang,Xuanping Li,Yanan Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PushDualGen是快手为大规模推送推荐系统设计的轻量级生成式推荐模型。它先生成语义ID（SID），再生成一段可跳过的解释文本，在实现端到端个性化推荐的同时保持可解释性，并大幅降低推理成本。该模型已在快手推送推荐系统中部署，线上A/B测试显示有效播放率相对提升8.50%，不满率相对下降37.70%，同时长期优化了内容生态，为长尾视频带来更多曝光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐虽能通过语义ID实现端到端个性化，但其黑盒特性导致推荐逻辑难以追溯，阻碍部署。OneRec-Thinking通过引入思维链（CoT）解决可解释性问题，但显著增加了推理成本，难以支撑近十亿用户的工业级推送场景。

Method: PushDualGen采用双阶段轻量级生成策略：首先由生成器输出语义ID完成推荐，随后生成一段可跳过的解释文本。通过共享生成器同时产出推荐结果与解释，避免OneRec-Thinking中完整CoT推理带来的高开销，在保持可解释性的同时实现轻量高效的推理。

Result: PushDualGen已在快手推送推荐系统大规模部署。线上A/B测试表明：用户观看视频的有效播放率相对提升8.50%，用户不满率相对下降37.70%。此外，长期来看该模型优化了内容生态，为长尾视频提供了更多曝光机会。

Conclusion: PushDualGen证明了在生成式推荐中加入轻量级可跳过解释的可行性，在工业级规模下成功平衡了推荐性能、可解释性与推理成本，验证了其在快手推送场景中的有效性，并为生成式推荐在大型工业系统的落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Push recommendation in KuaiShou proactively delivers personalized content to nearly one billion users to facilitate their engagement. Recently, generative recommendation has achieved end-to-end user personalization through semantic ID. However, their black- box characteristics make recommendation logics difficult to trace, hindering their deployment. OneRec-Thinking addresses this by incorporating CoT before generating SIDs, but this significantly increases inference cost. To support large-scale industrial applications, we propose PushDualGen, a lightweight generator, which first generates the SID and then produces a copy as a skippable explanation. PushDualGen has been deployed in Kuaishou's push recommendation system. Online A/B tests demonstrate the effectiveness of PushDualGen, delivering significant improvements in both user attraction and satisfaction. The effective play rate for videos recommended to users has relatively increased by 8.50%, while the dissatisfaction rate has relatively fallen by 37.70%. In the long term, PushDualGen optimises the content ecosystem, providing more exposure for long-tail videos.

</details>


### [3] [Give the Long-tail More SPACE: Promoting Provider Fairness in Next POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07998)
*Anran Zhang,Jiaqi Jiang,jiahui Jin,Yuhan Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SPACE是一个模型无关的框架，通过虚拟用户生成来提升POI推荐中的长尾商家曝光公平性，同时考虑用户执行约束和POI资源供给约束，在三个真实数据集上显著提升供应商公平性并保持甚至提升推荐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流POI推荐模型倾向于将曝光集中在少数热门POI上，导致长尾商家系统性地曝光不足。现有供应商公平性技术无法直接应用于POI推荐，因为用户面临执行约束（如地理位置可达性），POI面临资源供给约束（如容量限制）。

Method: SPACE包含三个阶段：(1) 社区推断以捕捉异构用户执行约束；(2) 非平衡最优传输分配，在POI特定供给预算下决定每个尾部POI应从哪些社区获得多少虚拟用户；(3) 约束引导的潜在扩散模型，生成POI条件、社区一致的虚拟用户嵌入。生成的用户-POI对可用于训练现有推荐器而无需修改其架构。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SPACE在多个骨干模型上显著提升了供应商公平性，同时保持甚至常常提升推荐准确率。

Conclusion: SPACE通过虚拟用户生成的方式，在不修改现有推荐器架构的前提下，实现了POI推荐中供应商公平性与推荐准确性的双赢，代码已公开。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) recommendation predicts users' future destinations from historical mobility sequences and has become a key component of location-based services. However, mainstream models often concentrate exposure on a small set of popular POIs, leaving long-tail merchants systematically under-exposed. While provider fairness has recently attracted increasing attention, directly applying existing provider-fairness techniques to POI recommendation is problematic: (i) users face execution constraints; and (ii) POIs face resource supply constraints. To address this, we propose SPACE (Supply- and Physics-Aware Conditional Embedding generation), a model-agnostic framework that improves long-tail POI exposure via virtual user generation under explicit feasibility and supply control. SPACE consists of three stages: (1) community inference to capture heterogeneous user execution constraints; (2) unbalanced optimal-transport allocation to decide how many virtual users each tail POI should receive from which communities under POI-specific supply budgets; and (3) constraint-guided latent diffusion to generate POI-conditional, community-consistent virtual user embeddings. The generated user-POI pairs can be seamlessly used to train existing recommenders without modifying their architectures. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that SPACE substantially improves provider fairness while maintaining and often improving recommendation accuracy across multiple backbone models. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Anniran1/SPACE-main.

</details>


### [4] [Search over the Visual World: Persistent Visual Memory, Layered Indexes, and Source-Grounded Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08075)
*Sankalp Nagaonkar,Rohit Garg,Ankit Raj,Ashish Choithani,Ashutosh Trivedi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向视觉世界（摄像头、屏幕、流媒体、档案）的搜索基础设施模型，将视频检索从"排名文件"重新定义为基于"场景"、"视觉记忆"和"能力声明索引"的持续观察与证据溯源问题。作者实现了VideoDB数据格式（VDB），并通过与商业视频原生引擎的对比实验（9800+查询）证明：通用组件管道在Recall@1/@3/@10上超过视频原生引擎，说明当前视觉检索的质量更多由系统设计而非视频专用预训练决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数视频检索系统假设有界语料库并返回排名文件或时间戳，但运行于摄像头、屏幕、流和档案之上的智能体面临不同的系统问题：观察持续到达、模型以不同时间粒度解释、上下文选择不能重放完整视觉记录、结果必须连接到可检查的源证据。现有方法无法解决这些基础设施层面的挑战。

Method: 提出一个概念化和形式化的视觉世界搜索模型，核心要素包括：分析器定义的场景（analyzer-defined scenes）、持久理解工件（persistent understanding artifacts）、视觉记忆作为共享源时间上共存的场景空间（coexisting scene spaces over shared source time）、能力声明索引（capability-declared indexes）。区分记忆（保留一切）、上下文（为任务选择的内容）和证据（支撑任务的源区间）。VideoDB数据格式（VDB）在生产中实现该模型，支持计划检索、状态调查、直接访问和基于证据的综合等类型化搜索界面。

Result: 在语义检索对比中，涵盖9800+查询、四个公开数据集，通用组件管道在宏观平均Recall@1/@3/@10上达到73.09/83.39/91.20，高于商业视频原生引擎的65.75/77.13/89.10；而基线在Recall@50上更高（96.42对比96.07）。

Conclusion: 视觉世界的检索质量当前更多由系统架构设计而非视频专用预训练决定；视觉记忆基础设施可以在保持可播放的、源有据的证据为一等公民的同时实现高质量检索。

Abstract: Most video-retrieval systems assume a bounded corpus and return ranked files or timestamps. Agents operating over cameras, screens, streams, and archives face a different systems problem: observations arrive continuously; models interpret them at different temporal granularities; context must be selected without replaying the complete visual record; and results must stay connected to inspectable source evidence. We argue that search over such a corpus is an infrastructure problem that cannot be reduced to ranking video files. We develop a conceptual and formal model of search over the visual world built on analyzer-defined scenes, persistent understanding artifacts, visual memory as coexisting scene spaces over shared source time, and capability-declared indexes, distinguishing memory (everything retained), context (what is selected for a task), and evidence (the source intervals that ground it). The VideoDB data format (VDB) realizes this model in production, exposed through a typed search surface spanning planned retrieval, stateful investigation, direct access, and grounded synthesis. We contrast this model-agnostic infrastructure, where segmentation, sampling, model choice, embeddings, and ranking are system decisions and live streams are first-class sources, with video-native foundation models offered as fixed APIs. In a semantic-retrieval comparison against a commercial video-native engine spanning 9,800+ queries over four public datasets, a pipeline of general-purpose components achieves higher macro-averaged Recall@1/@3/@10 (73.09/83.39/91.20 versus 65.75/77.13/89.10), while the baseline is higher at Recall@50 (96.42 versus 96.07). Retrieval quality over the visual world is today governed more by system design than by video-specific pretraining, and visual-memory infrastructure can deliver it while keeping playable, source-grounded evidence first-class.

</details>


### [5] [DS@GT ARC at Touché: Large Language Models for Retrieval-Augmented Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08143)
*Anthony Miyaguchi,Conor Johnston*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文扩展了DS@GT ARC团队在Touché 2025检索增强辩论任务上的工作，使用六个来自三家提供商的领先LLM，通过检索增强提示流水线完成两个子任务：辩论下一句话生成和按格赖斯准则（量、质、关系、方式）评估辩论回复。分析发现前沿LLM是强响应生成器，作为评估器时在模型家族内部高度一致，但这种共识并不能可靠预测官方评估结果，尤其在"质"准则上差距最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多LLM评估器之间的一致性是否可以作为官方评估性能的可靠代理指标，因为LLM评估器的群体共识并不一定等同于任务的实际质量标准。

Method: 使用六个来自三家提供商的领先LLM，通过检索增强提示流水线参与两个子任务（辩论响应生成和格赖斯准则评估），并分析多LLM评估器一致性是否与官方评估结果一致。

Result: 前沿LLM系统是强大的响应生成器；作为评估器时，同一模型家族内部评估一致性很强，但这种群体共识不能可靠追踪官方评估目标，其中在Quality准则上的差距最大。

Conclusion: 多LLM评估器一致性不能作为官方评估性能的可靠代理，尤其是在Quality准则上，需要谨慎依赖LLM群体共识进行系统评估。

Abstract: We extend the DS@GT ARC working-note submission to the Touché 2025 Retrieval-Augmented Debate task. The task has two subtasks: generating the next utterance in a simulated debate, and evaluating debate responses according to the Gricean maxims of Quantity, Quality, Relation, and Manner. The DS@GT ARC submission consisted of six leading LLMs from three providers through a retrieval-augmented prompting pipeline. We summarize the results from the working paper and explore whether multi-LLM evaluator agreement is a reliable proxy for official evaluation performance. The analysis shows that frontier LLM systems are strong response generators, and as evaluators they agree strongly within model families. However this consensus does not reliably track the official evaluation target, with the largest gap on the Quality maxim. The accompanying source code for this paper is located at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/touche-2025-rad and https://github.com/dsgt-arc/touche-2025-rad-analysis.

</details>


### [6] [Personalized Communication Skills for Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08417)
*Zongwei Wang,Min Gao,Guangyu Hu,Xinyi Gao,Junliang Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AgentCom 是一个用于智能体推荐系统的个性化沟通技能框架，通过引入其他用户作为顾问智能体来缓解用户智能体孤立推理导致的视角狭窄问题，并以"为什么-什么-如何-谁"技能库加个性化路由和失败驱动进化的方式实现自适应通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的用户智能体在评估候选物品时通常基于有限的历史记录进行孤立推理，导致视角狭窄：仅从局部不完整的视角评估候选，忽略相关的偏好侧面，产生不准确的判断。引入其他用户作为顾问可以提供互补证据，但通用的用户-顾问通信流程不充分，因为不同用户的决策状态需要不同形式的外部建议。

Method: 提出 AgentCom 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 共享的"为什么-什么-如何-谁"技能库（why 识别决策缺陷，what 指定信息任务，how 确定顾问交互协议，who 检索能执行该协议的顾问）；(2) 个性化技能路由，按用户和推荐情境顺序选择合适技能构建通信路径；(3) 失败驱动技能进化，从失败通信案例中学习并丰富技能库以覆盖未解决的通信需求。

Result: 实验表明 AgentCom 在传统推荐器、社交推荐器和智能体推荐器上均一致地提升了推荐性能。

Conclusion: 将外部顾问引入用户智能体评估过程，并通过结构化的技能库和个性化/自适应机制来组织通信，能有效缓解视角狭窄问题，提升推荐准确度，且具有跨推荐器类型的通用性。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems increasingly employ large language model-based UserAgents to evaluate candidate items through simulated feedback before recommendations are delivered. However, existing UserAgents typically reason in isolation based on limited personal histories, which may lead to perspective narrowing: the agent evaluates candidates from a local and incomplete view, overlooks relevant preference facets, and consequently produces inaccurate judgments. A natural way to alleviate this problem is to introduce other users as advisor agents, whose diverse histories provide complementary evidence that helps the target user reconsider overlooked preference signals. Nevertheless, a generic user-advisor communication process is insufficient, as different user decision states require different forms of external advice. Based on this insight, we propose AgentCom, a personalized communication skill framework for agentic recommender systems. AgentCom organizes reusable communication skills into a shared why--what--how--who skill bank: why identifies the decision deficiency that necessitates communication, what specifies the information task, how determines the advisor interaction protocol, and who retrieves advisors capable of executing that protocol. To make the shared skill bank personalized at use time and adaptive over time, AgentCom introduces two complementary mechanisms: personalized skill routing and failure-driven skill evolution. Personalized skill routing constructs a communication path by sequentially selecting suitable skills for each user and recommendation context. Failure-driven skill evolution learns from unsuccessful communication cases and enriches the shared bank with reusable skills that address previously uncovered communication needs. Experiments show that AgentCom consistently improves recommendation performance across traditional, social, and agentic recommenders.

</details>


### [7] [Structure-Preserving Projection for Mitigating Modality Bias in LLM-Based Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08583)
*Tzu-Wei Chiu,Song-Duo Ma,Hsin-Yu Lin,Pu-Jen Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构保持投影方法，用于解决LLM推荐系统中将协同嵌入投影到LLM嵌入空间时引入的模态偏差问题，通过专用的结构保持损失来维持协同嵌入的关系几何结构，实验证明该方法能持续提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM推荐系统将协同嵌入投影到LLM的嵌入空间以整合文本和协同信号，但这种投影会引入模态偏差，扭曲底层的协同结构，限制了投影嵌入的有效性。

Method: 提出一种结构保持投影方法，通过专用的结构保持损失来维护协同嵌入的关系几何结构，从而在投影过程中保留协同信号的原始结构信息。

Result: 综合实验表明，该方法能够持续改进推荐性能，为LLM推荐提供了更可靠的路径。

Conclusion: 结构保持投影是解决LLM推荐中模态偏差问题的有效方案，能够更好地整合文本与协同信号，提升推荐系统的整体表现。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based recommenders integrate textual and collaborative signals by projecting collaborative embeddings into the embedding space of the LLM. However, this projection can introduce modality bias that distorts the underlying collaborative structure and limits the usefulness of projected embeddings. To address this issue, we propose a novel structure-preserving projection approach that maintains the relational geometry of collaborative embeddings through dedicated structure-preserving losses. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently improves recommendation performance, providing a more reliable path for LLM-based recommendation.

</details>


### [8] [BOUND: Brief-Guided Corrective Preference Distillation at Search-Control Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08768)
*Qingying Niu,Ruiyang Ren,Wayne Xin Zhao,Yaliang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BOUND是一个基于简要引导的纠正性偏好蒸馏框架，用于解决LLM深度搜索智能体中的持续性搜索漂移问题。它通过构建教师侧的搜索状态简要信息来区分任务对齐的延续与局部合理的漂移延续，并利用DPO将纠正性偏好蒸馏到学生模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM深度搜索智能体在迭代检索与推理过程中，局部相关的证据会导致持续的错误锚点漂移、约束漂移或局部主题漂移。现有方法监督轨迹、结果或步骤，但很少区分任务对齐的延续与局部合理但会强化漂移的延续。

Method: BOUND为每个学生诱导的决策时状态构建教师侧搜索状态简要信息，保留原始搜索目标和关键约束，同时总结已确认证据、缺失信息和漂移状态。教师根据简要信息判断学生的延续是否包含可纠正的局部搜索控制错误，结合rollout结果构建纠正对比（学生特异性修正vs原始延续）或终止对比（有支持的答案vs不必要的检索延续）。每个验证的状态匹配偏好对操作化搜索控制边界，通过DPO蒸馏到学生中，而简要信息和教师侧计算仅限训练阶段。

Result: BOUND在四个多跳QA基准和三个深度搜索基准上评估。在重跑基线的六个基准中，BOUND在五个数据集和14个指标中的12个上领先。在同一搜索控制接口和匹配设置下，BOUND在Bamboogle上比Trajectory SFT高出5.6个EM点，在BrowseComp-Plus上高出4.8个准确率点。

Conclusion: BOUND通过简要引导的纠正性偏好蒸馏有效解决搜索漂移问题，相比现有SFT方法显著提升了深度搜索智能体的表现，展示了状态匹配的偏好对在定义搜索控制边界方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based deep search agents solve tasks through iterative retrieval and reasoning, but locally relevant evidence can cause persistent wrong-anchor drift, constraint drift, or local-topic drift. Existing methods supervise trajectories, outcomes, or steps, but rarely distinguish task-aligned continuations from locally plausible ones that reinforce drift. We propose BOUND, a brief-guided corrective preference distillation framework for persistent search drift. For each student-induced decision-time state, BOUND constructs a teacher-side search-state brief that preserves the original search target and key constraints while summarizing confirmed evidence, missing information, and drift status. Guided by the brief, the teacher determines whether the student's continuation contains a correctable local search-control error likely to affect subsequent decisions. Together with the rollout outcome, this assessment determines whether to construct a corrective contrast between a student-specific correction and the original continuation, or a termination contrast between a supported answer and an unnecessary retrieval continuation. Each validated state-matched preference pair operationalizes a search-control boundary. Direct preference optimization (DPO) distills these preferences into the student, while the brief and teacher-side computation remain confined to training. We evaluate BOUND on four multi-hop QA benchmarks and three deep-search benchmarks. Across the six benchmarks for which we reran baselines, BOUND leads on five datasets and 12 of 14 metrics. Under the same search-control interface and matched settings, BOUND outperforms Trajectory SFT by 5.6 EM points on Bamboogle and 4.8 accuracy points on BrowseComp-Plus. Code is available at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/BOUND.

</details>

*Jiuyi Zheng,Gan Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对一个固定面板中的六个分层过程语料库进行了对称审计，考察内容近重复层、公共容器层及其并集的组件结构，发现MyFixit和Doc2Dial表现出"单层通过/并集失败"的模式，但该模式无法区分于并集密度控制，因此未识别出桥特异性机制。论文贡献在于有界测量与审计，而非新算法或一般性因果主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组件不相交泄漏控制可通过内容相似度、层级隶属关系或两者结合对语料单元进行分组。先前工作已表明合并关系类型可产生阻碍分割的巨型组件，本文希望以对称审计的方式审视该现象，而非将其作为新发现。

Method: 对六个分层过程语料库组成的固定面板，分别对内容近重复层、公共容器层以及两者的并集进行对称的组件结构审计；使用预设的桥特异性预测因子与已注册的并集密度控制进行对比；并进行阈值敏感性、标注覆盖率和词汇线索的次级诊断。

Result: MyFixit和Doc2Dial在相同操作标准下表现出"单层通过/并集失败"模式，比例为六分之二；桥特异性预测因子与模式相关，但无法区别于并集密度控制；次级诊断为阈值敏感性和词汇线索提供了有界解释。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是受界限定的测量与审计：保持关系族可见、对称评估各层及并集的组件结构、报告负例与机制局限。论文不提出新的分割算法，也不对关系并集提出一般性因果主张。

Abstract: Component-disjoint leakage control can group corpus units by content similarity, hierarchical membership, or both. Guvenilir and Dogan previously showed that merging relation types can create a giant component that obstructs splitting; we do not claim this phenomenon as new. We examine it through a symmetric audit of a content-near-duplicate layer, a common-container layer, and their union in a fixed panel of six hierarchical procedural corpora. MyFixit and Doc2Dial exhibit the individual-layer-pass/union-fail pattern under the same operational criteria. The resulting two-of-six fraction describes this deliberately constructed panel and is not a prevalence estimate. A prespecified bridge-specific predictor is associated with the pattern, but it is not distinguished from a registered union-density control; the panel therefore does not identify a bridge-specific mechanism. Secondary diagnostics bound the interpretation of threshold sensitivity, annotation coverage, and lexical cues without extending those findings beyond their recorded sources and definitions. The paper's contribution is a bounded measurement and audit: it keeps relation families visible, evaluates their individual and union component structures symmetrically, and reports negative cases and mechanism limits. It proposes neither a new splitting algorithm nor a general causal claim about relation unions.

</details>


### [10] [Difficulty-Gated Fusion of Reasoning Views for Temporal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08940)
*Jamie Holdcroft,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种查询难度门控的融合方法（query-difficulty-gated fusion），用于时序推理信息检索。通过将查询扩展为多个显式化时间意图的重写文本并分别检索，再基于八维分数分布特征和约千参数的轻量门控网络为每个查询动态分配各视图权重，实现无需相关标签、无需重排序、无需微调检索器的融合排序。在Tempo基准上，该方法使六种检索器（从BERT到7B解码器）均有提升，最强检索器达到0.297和0.303 nDCG@10，且每查询增益显著（p<0.001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序推理检索任务中，查询与文档的相关性依赖于共享的时序推理而非词面重叠。将查询重写为多个明确时间意图的变体并进行检索能提供所需推理，但使用等权融合会损失准确率——因为对任意单一查询，只有部分重写变体是可靠的。现有方法未能针对不同查询自适应选择可靠的重写视图，因此需要一种能按查询动态分配视图权重的方法。

Method: 该方法包括：(1) 将原始查询扩展为多个显式时间意图的重写文本作为不同"推理视图"；(2) 对每个视图提取八维分数分布签名，涵盖查询性能预测指标（softmax熵、分数差距、离散度等）；(3) 训练一个约一千参数的轻量门控网络，将签名映射为每查询的视图权重；(4) 推理时使用加权融合排序，无需相关标签、无需重排序、不微调检索器；(5) 门控网络采用leave-one-task-out方式训练。

Result: 在Tempo基准上，该方法使所有六种检索器（从BERT编码器到7B解码器检索器）均获得提升，对较弱骨干检索器的提升最大。最强检索器达到0.297和0.303的nDCG@10；与原始查询相比，每查询增益在配对bootstrap检验下显著（p<0.001）。每查询oracle达到0.364，而实际实现为0.297，揭示了性能提升空间，指出每查询视图选择是可进一步探索的方向。

Conclusion: 查询难度门控融合为时序推理检索提供了一种高效、无需额外标注的自适应融合策略。该方法验证了轻量门控网络利用分数分布统计特征即可有效识别可靠视图的可行性，同时揭示了per-query视图选择仍是具有较大改进空间（0.297 vs 0.364 oracle）的关键下一步方向。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive temporal retrieval requires matching a query to documents whose relevance depends on shared temporal reasoning rather than lexical overlap. Expanding a query into several reformulations that make its temporal intent explicit, and retrieving with each, supplies this reasoning, but fusing the resulting rankings with equal weights wastes accuracy: for any single query, only some reformulations are reliable. We propose query-difficulty-gated fusion of reasoning views. From each view we read an eight-dimensional signature of its score distribution, built from query-performance-prediction quantities such as softmax entropy, score gaps, and dispersion, and a gate of roughly one thousand parameters maps these signatures to per-query view weights. The fused ranking uses no relevance labels at inference, no re-ranking, and no fine-tuning of the retriever; the gate is trained leave-one-task-out. On the \textsc{Tempo} benchmark, the method improves all six retrievers we evaluate, from BERT encoders to 7B decoder retrievers, with the largest gains on the weaker backbones. The strongest retrievers reach $0.297$ and $0.303$ nDCG@10, and the per-query gain over the original query is significant under a paired bootstrap ($p<0.001$). A per-query oracle reaches $0.364$ against our realized $0.297$, exposing headroom that identifies per-query view selection as a concrete next step.

</details>


### [11] [What Would Fix This RAG Failure? Auditing Counterfactual Response with Paired Evidence Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08944)
*Wenzhang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Pair-ID，一种离线审计方法，用于测量RAG（检索增强生成）失败答案在对证据进行修复（添加缺失支持、删除已验证的不支持内容）后可能产生的反事实响应。通过19,981个查询的完整漏斗筛选，对1,190个有效失败案例进行修复实验，发现证据修复的修复率有限（支持添加0.328，删除0.136），且修复结果仅部分可预测、高度依赖读者模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统的失败答案可能对应多种不同的、尚未被观察到的证据修复方案。作者希望理解这些问题在多大程度上可以通过证据修复来解决，以及失败响应是否携带预测修复结果的信号。这关系到能否建立对RAG失败修复的可靠性评估框架。

Method: 采用离线审计框架Pair-ID：固定查询、检索状态和阅读器不变，交叉两种证据操作（添加缺失支持证据、删除已验证的不支持证据），测量同失败反事实响应向量。通过完整漏斗从19,981个基准查询筛选出11,105个符合条件的Qwen失败，用SHA-256排序前瞻性地预选1,200个案例，最终获得1,190个再生成有效的失败案例。用长度和位置匹配的假对照（shams）验证语义对比度，并评估原始失败响应对修复的预测能力。

Result: 支持添加修复了197/600 JOINT案例（0.328，95% CI [0.292, 0.367]），删除修复了162/1,190案例（0.136，95% CI [0.117, 0.155]）；长度和位置匹配的假对照保留0.223和0.101的语义对比度。原始视图对个体响应单元的预测信号有限（macro AUROC 0.678；Brier 0.152 vs 0.160基线），精确向量准确率0.637低于多数向量基线0.646，向量macro-F1仅0.170。跨四个读者，边际敏感性重现但精确向量一致性仅0.675-0.765，JOINT-only一致性降至0.538-0.691。

Conclusion: 证据敏感性在哈希选择的合格失败样本中以有意义比例发生，但只能从观察到的失败中对修复结果进行部分预测，且修复效果高度依赖具体读者模型。该工作支持的是"框架限定的离线响应审计"这一定位，而非信息论不可能性结论、读者无关的分类体系或运行时修复策略。

Abstract: A failed retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) answer can be consistent with several unseen responses to evidence repair. We introduce Pair-ID, an offline audit that holds one query, retrieval state, and reader constant, then crosses two operations, adding missing support and deleting verified nonsupport, to measure a same-failure counterfactual response vector. A complete funnel over 19,981 benchmark queries identifies 11,105 eligible Qwen failures, from which a prospectively fixed SHA-256 ordering selects 1,200 before generating any sampled response. Among 1,190 regenerated-valid failures, support addition repairs 197/600 JOINT cases (0.328, 95% CI [0.292, 0.367]), and deletion repairs 162/1,190 cases (0.136, 95% CI [0.117, 0.155]); length- and position-matched shams retain semantic contrasts of 0.223 and 0.101. The original view carries partial predictive signal for individual response cells (macro AUROC 0.678; Brier 0.152 versus 0.160 for a marginal baseline), but exact-vector accuracy, 0.637, does not exceed the 0.646 majority-vector baseline, and vector macro-F1 is 0.170. Across four readers, both marginal sensitivities recur, while pooled exact-vector agreement is 0.675-0.765 and JOINT-only agreement falls to 0.538-0.691. These results show that evidence sensitivity occurs at meaningful rates in the hash-selected eligible-failure sample, is only partially predictable from the observed failure, and is conditional on the reader. The evidence supports a frame-scoped offline response audit, not an information-theoretic impossibility result, reader-independent taxonomy, or runtime repair policy.

</details>


### [12] [Guardian Crawler: Retrieval-First Knowledge Discovery with Bounded LLM Augmentation for Noisy Web Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08994)
*Joshua Castillo,Santosh Nukavarapu,Ravi Mukkamala*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Guardian Crawler，一个可复现的检索优先测试平台，用于在合成网页语料上进行知识发现和证据接地摘要的受控实验。该系统结合BM25检索与风险感知、嵌入增强及混合重排序，并采用带显式文献引用的约束RAG生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从嘈杂的网页数据中检索相关证据具有挑战性，尤其是在涉及不完整报告、异质语言和无关内容的敏感领域。现有研究缺乏可复现、可控的测试平台来进行知识发现和证据接地摘要的实验。

Method: Guardian Crawler架构包括：(1) BM25检索作为基础组件；(2) 风险感知、嵌入增强和混合重排序策略；(3) 带显式文档引用的约束检索增强生成。在包含900篇文档的合成语料库和10个查询上进行实验评估。

Result: 风险感知重排序取得最佳描述性检索分数（P@10=1.00, NDCG@10=0.94），优于BM25（P@10=0.94, NDCG@10=0.81）。最佳混合配置NDCG@10=0.94，BM25+语义配置NDCG@10=0.88。41个可评估生成的要点全部通过词法覆盖阈值，LLM法官判定36个得到支持、1个部分支持、4个不支持。

Conclusion: Guardian Crawler作为受控测试平台是可行的，但研究尚未建立统计优越性、人工验证的忠实性或对实时网络调查环境的可迁移性，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Retrieving relevant evidence from noisy web data is challenging, particularly in sensitive domains containing incomplete reports, heterogeneous language, and irrelevant content. We present Guardian Crawler, a reproducible retrieval-first testbed for controlled experiments on knowledge discovery and evidence-grounded summarization over synthetic web-like corpora. The architecture combines BM25 retrieval with risk-aware, embedding-augmented, and hybrid reranking, followed by constrained retrieval-augmented generation with explicit document citations. Experiments on a synthetic 900-document corpus and 10 queries produced the highest descriptive retrieval scores under risk-based reranking, with P@10 = 1.00 and NDCG@10 = 0.94, compared with 0.94 and 0.81 for BM25. The best hybrid and BM25+Semantic configurations reached NDCG@10 values of 0.94 and 0.88, respectively. All 41 evaluable generated bullets passed the lexical coverage threshold; an automated LLM judge classified 36 as supported, one as partially supported, and four as unsupported. These results demonstrate the feasibility of Guardian Crawler as a controlled testbed but do not establish statistical superiority, human-validated faithfulness, or transfer to live-web investigative environments.

</details>


### [13] [PreGress: Ranking-Native Pre-training and Prompting for Graph Node Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09016)
*Lujie Ban,Jiasheng shi,Yingli Zhou,Kaiwen Xue,Daiyin Wang,Xubin Li,Shuanghua Li,Chenhao Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了PreGress框架，这是首个面向节点排序的原生预训练与提示框架，通过多任务预训练（度中心性预测和属性重构）结合轻量级任务特定提示模块，在不进行全量重训练的情况下适应多种节点排序任务，在六个公开图数据集和两个真实基准上验证了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图上的精确排名计算在大规模场景下计算代价过高；现有GNN排序方法通常针对单一排序准则设计，需要为每个下游任务重训练，可迁移性和效率受限；现有的图预训练方法的学习目标与节点排序任务不对齐，导致排序应用的适应性不佳。

Method: PreGress采用多任务预训练策略，设计两个目标：度中心性预测和属性重构，以联合捕获图的结构信息和属性信息；同时设计轻量级的任务特定提示模块，使冻结的排序骨干网络能够通过提示适配不同的下游排序任务，无需全量重训练。

Result: 在六个公开图数据集、两个真实世界查询到物品基准（Yelp2018和MovieLens-100K）以及受控的五准则图访问研究中，PreGress展示了强大的排序质量，同时保持了较低的任务特定状态开销。

Conclusion: PreGress验证了排序原生的预训练和提示框架能够有效支持广泛的节点排序任务，兼顾了排序性能与跨任务可迁移性，解决了图预训练与排序应用之间的目标错配问题。

Abstract: Node ranking is a fundamental problem in graph information retrieval, measuring the relative importance of nodes and supporting a wide range of applications such as influence analysis, recommendation, and graph-based retrieval augmented generation. However, exact computation of graph-based ranking measures is often computationally prohibitive at scale. Existing GNN-based ranking methods provide scalable approximations, but they are typically tailored to individual ranking criteria and require retraining for each downstream task, which limits their transferability and efficiency. Recent graph pre-training approaches aim to enable knowledge transfer across tasks, yet their learning objectives are largely misaligned with node ranking, resulting in suboptimal adaptability to ranking-oriented applications. To address these limitations, we propose PreGress, the first ranking-native pre-training and prompting framework for supporting a wide range of node ranking tasks. PreGress performs multi-task pre-training using our carefully designed objectives, including degree centrality prediction and attribute reconstruction, to jointly capture structural and attribute information. To support heterogeneous ranking criteria, we design lightweight, task-specific prompt modules that adapt a frozen ranking backbone to downstream tasks without full retraining. Experiments on six public graphs and two real-world query-to-item benchmarks---Yelp2018 and MovieLens-100K---together with a controlled five-criterion graph-access study demonstrate strong ranking quality with low task-specific state overhead.

</details>


### [14] [RVANNS: Mixed-Precision Indexing and Locality-Aware Graph Traversal on RISC-V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09077)
*Chengying Huan,Yudong Liu,Jianguo Wang,Lizheng Chen,Renling Yin,Weijia Chen,Ji Qi,Jiageng Yu,Junjie Xu,Jie Zhang,Chen Tian,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RVANNS，一个面向RISC-V向量扩展（RVV）的近似最近邻搜索引擎，通过混合精度多层索引（MPMI）和重排序邻接表（ROrder）联合优化向量表示与图局部性，在真实128位和256位RVV处理器上分别实现3.39x和4.94x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU上的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）性能越来越受限于候选向量移动和解码开销，而非峰值算术吞吐量。RVV虽提供向量长度无关执行和LMUL寄存器分组，但通用低精度解码存在转换开销，且不规则图遍历产生的分散访问损害缓存局部性和内存级并行度。

Method: 1) 混合精度多层索引（MPMI）：每个向量用密集8位仿射基+稀疏FP16/FP32残差表示，融合重建与距离累加，对齐加宽操作与LMUL大小的寄存器组；2) ROrder：将可能共同访问的图节点共置，并对重映射的邻接表排序，将分散的载荷探测转换为更密集、主要向前移动的地址流。

Result: 集成到Milvus后，在真实128位和256位RVV处理器上较标量执行分别获得3.39x和4.94x加速；在受控HNSW配置下，比RVV SIMD+FP32提升2.27-2.76x吞吐量，比AVX-512和SVE基线提升1.18-1.59x；在Cohere10M数据集上比GPU基线获得1.82-2.27x更高的QPS/W。

Conclusion: RVANNS证明了在RVV架构上通过联合优化向量表示精度和图遍历局部性，可以显著超越标量和传统SIMD基线，甚至在能效上优于GPU方案，为新兴RISC-V处理器上的ANNS提供了高效设计范式。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) on CPUs is increasingly constrained by candidate-vector movement and decoding rather than peak arithmetic throughput. Although the RISC-V Vector Extension (RVV) provides vector-length-agnostic execution and LMUL-based register grouping, generic low-precision decoding still incurs conversion overhead, while irregular graph traversal generates scattered accesses that degrade cache locality and memory-level parallelism.
  We present RVANNS, an RVV-oriented ANNS engine that jointly optimizes vector representation and graph locality. Its Mixed-Precision Multi-Layer Index (MPMI) represents each vector with a dense 8-bit affine base and sparse FP16/FP32 residuals, fusing reconstruction with distance accumulation and aligning widening with LMUL-sized register groups. ROrder co-locates likely co-visited graph nodes and sorts remapped adjacency lists, transforming scattered payload probes into denser, predominantly forward-moving address streams.
  Integrated into Milvus, RVANNS achieves 3.39x and 4.94x speedups over scalar execution on real 128-bit and 256-bit RVV processors, respectively. Under controlled HNSW configurations, it improves throughput by 2.27--2.76x over RVV SIMD+FP32 and by 1.18--1.59x over the corresponding AVX-512 and SVE baselines. On Cohere10M, it further delivers 1.82--2.27x higher QPS/W than the evaluated GPU baselines.

</details>


### [15] [DREAM Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09408)
*Bin Zhang,Bowen Zheng,Chao Yi,Chengyu Lai,Dian Chen,Dimin Wang,Gaoyang Guo,Jialin Zhu,Jian Wu,Jing Yu,Jiuning Lin,Lingqing Zhang,Lingyun Zheng,Mao Zhang,Mingming Pan,Ruiquan Lan,Shuai Zhong,Wen Chen,Wendong Zhang,Xiaodong Zhu,Xuan Chen,Xunke Xi,Yifan Lu,Yiheng Wang,Yue Zeng,Yujie Luo,Yuning Jiang,Zhe Hu,Zhibo Xiao,Zihong Huang,Binbin Cao,Bo Zheng,Danning Wang,Dixuan Wang,Ge Fan,Haixia Wu,Han Zhu,Hao Fang,Haoming Chen,Huiping Chu,Jian Wang,Jianjun Wu,Jiawei Wu,Jiaxin Yu,Jingwen Liu,Jinzhe Shan,Kai Meng,Kai Zhang,Keqin Xu,Kewei Zhu,Lang Tian,Leihui Chen,Li Chen,Licheng Xu,Lide Xiao,Ruitong Zhang,Shiyao Peng,Silu Zhou,Tao Wang,Wei Shi,Wenjun Yang,Xiang Chen,Xiang Gao,Xiao Ren,Xu Liu,Xuwen Wang,Yang Li,Yeqiu Yang,Yi Hu,Yinnan Song,Yuan Liu,Yunqi Gao,Zhiliang Huang,Zhujin Gao,Zongyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM（基于智能体方法的推荐引擎开发），一种自主优化控制架构，在不替换现有级联检索-排序-重排推荐流水线的前提下，通过添加感知层、可编排的策略层来解决会话级意图漂移（浏览/比较/购买）问题，并在淘宝首页信息流上取得显著业务指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统普遍采用级联式检索-排序-重排流水线，虽然高效但存在三大问题：信息与目标在模块间碎片化、依赖刚性规则、对实时意图感知有限，导致浏览、比较、购买等会话级意图转换没有被充分处理。

Method: DREAM包含两大核心组件：（1）三层意图引擎（Intent Engine），融合端侧信号为L0/L1/L2结构化意图表示，通过边缘-云端触发链将上报量降低至约8.7%；（2）元引擎（Meta Engine），使用MetaModel进行M1→M2→M3分层推理（意图总结、策略规划、参数翻译），通过统一出口和安全管理机制下发参数。此外，奖励双循环（Reward Dual Loop）结合离线模拟（策略空间探索）与在线反馈（结果校准）持续优化两组件。

Result: 淘宝首页信息流大规模A/B测试显示：仅控制重排模块即可使IPV提升2.06%、Core IPV提升2.39%、GMV提升0.88%；扩展控制到精排后，收益提升至2.71%、3.06%和1.31%，同时PV持续提升超过1%。这些收益不需要替换流水线模型或牺牲服务稳定性。

Conclusion: DREAM验证了智能体元控制作为工业推荐系统可行范式的有效性，能够在不破坏现有流水线的前提下实现端到端的目标优化，为工业推荐系统提供了一种可审计、可编排、可感知的上层控制方案。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems commonly use cascaded retrieval, ranking, and re-ranking pipelines. Although efficient, these pipelines fragment information and objectives across modules, rely on rigid rules, and have limited awareness of real-time intent, leaving session-level shifts among browsing, comparison, and purchase insufficiently addressed. We present DREAM (Developing Recommender Engine with Agentic Methods), an autonomous optimization control architecture that adds a perception-aware, orchestrable, and auditable policy layer atop existing pipelines without replacing them. DREAM has two core components. First, a three-tier Intent Engine fuses on-device signals into structured L0/L1/L2 intent representations; its edge-cloud trigger chain reduces reporting volume to approximately 8.7%. Second, a Meta Engine uses a MetaModel for layered M1-to-M2-to-M3 reasoning: intent summarization, strategy planning informed by Strategy Memory, and parameter translation. It dispatches the resulting parameters through a unified outlet with safety guardrails. A Reward Dual Loop continuously optimizes both components by combining offline simulation for strategy-space exploration with online feedback for outcome calibration, forming a cycle of generation, execution, evaluation, and experience accumulation. Large-scale A/B tests on Taobao's homepage feed show that re-ranking control alone improves IPV by 2.06%, Core IPV by 2.39%, and GMV by 0.88%. Extending control to fine ranking raises these gains to 2.71%, 3.06%, and 1.31%, respectively, while consistently improving PV by more than 1%. These gains require neither replacement of pipeline models nor compromise of serving stability, supporting agentic meta-control as a viable paradigm for industrial recommendation.

</details>


### [16] [MetaStrategy: Generative Ranking with Executable LLM Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09440)
*Chengyu Lai,Jiuning Lin,Zhibo Xiao,Xiaodong Zhu,Ruiquan Lan,Bin Zhang,Zihong Huang,Wendong Zhang,Chuxin Chen,Yinjiang Cai,Shuai Zhong,Lingqing Zhang,Dimin Wang,Jialin Zhu,Han Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MetaStrategy是一个工业推荐系统框架，通过让大语言模型生成结构化、可执行的排序策略（而非直接生成商品序列），在Generator-Evaluator架构下与现有预测模型、运营规则和防护机制集成。该框架在淘宝首页"猜你喜欢"场景部署，离线训练使用生产路径重放环境，线上A/B测试中点击PV提升2.11%、IPV提升3.12%、交易额提升2.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式排序方法通常直接构造商品序列，难以与成熟的预测模型、运营规则和字段级防护机制集成。工业推荐系统需要在多种异构目标（用户、商业、体验等）下排序异构内容，因此需要一种既能利用生成模型的优势、又能与现有基础设施无缝集成的方案。

Method: 1) LLM策略在请求上下文条件下输出类型化JSON配置（包含目标权重、内容/类别偏好、体验约束和位置策略）；2) 确定性验证器和编译器实例化隔离的Generator，与现有排序器在list级Evaluator下原子竞争；3) 在生产路径重放环境中训练策略（无用户暴露）；4) 结合选择、相对排序和基线提升三种奖励，采用自竞争课程学习；5) Evaluator路由的奖励增强在线策略蒸馏，将4B参数学师模型知识迁移到0.8B学生模型；6) 通过diff触发的近线生成部署，LLM推理在同步排序之外。

Result: 在淘宝首页"猜你喜欢"七天的用户随机在线A/B测试中：MetaStrategy在27.93%的治疗侧GE调用中获胜，点击PV提升2.11%，商品详情页浏览量(IPV)提升3.12%，交易金额提升2.83%，且响应时间无显著增加。

Conclusion: 通过生成结构化排名策略而非直接生成商品序列，MetaStrategy成功将LLM生成能力与工业推荐系统的预测模型、运营规则和防护机制相结合，在实际生产中取得了显著的商业指标提升，验证了"策略生成"范式在大规模工业推荐系统中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems rank heterogeneous content under coupled user, business, commercial, and experience objectives. Existing generative ranking methods typically construct item sequences directly, making them difficult to integrate with mature predictive models, operational rules, and field-level guardrails. We present MetaStrategy, a framework that instead generates a structured, executable ranking strategy. Conditioned on request context, a large language model (LLM) policy emits a typed JSON bundle controlling objective weights, content and category preferences, experience constraints, and position policies. A deterministic validator and compiler instantiate an isolated Generator that competes atomically with incumbents under the list-level Evaluator of the Generator-Evaluator (GE) architecture. We train the policy in a production-path replay environment that re-executes logged requests through the current re-ranking stack without user exposure. The method combines selection, relative-rank, and baseline-lift rewards, a self-competitive curriculum that feeds frequent strategies back as competitors, and Evaluator-routed reward-augmented on-policy distillation that transfers complementary 4B-parameter Teachers into a compact 0.8B-parameter Student. We deploy MetaStrategy in Taobao Homepage Guess You Like through diff-triggered nearline generation; LLM inference remains outside synchronous ranking, with no observable increase in response time (RT). In a seven-day user-randomized online A/B test, MetaStrategy wins 27.93% of treatment-side GE calls and significantly improves click page views (click PV) by 2.11%, item-detail page views (IPV) by 3.12%, and transaction amount by 2.83%.

</details>


### [17] [TSPORec: Token Selection via Preference Optimization for LLM-Based Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09605)
*Wenqiao Zhu,Chao Xu,Haipang Wu,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了TSPORec，一种用于LLM-based序列推荐系统的Token选择方法，通过精准定位全文中有信息量的token来提升推荐性能，同时降低推理成本，实验显示性能提升最高31.25%，效率提升最高63.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based序列推荐系统虽然性能优越，但大规模深层架构导致推理成本高昂，投资回报率低。现有方法为降低成本只使用物品描述的前几个token，丢失了全文中的有价值信息，导致推荐性能次优。

Method: 提出TSPORec方法，设计了三阶段流水线来筛选信息量高的token，并引入新颖的代理奖励(proxy reward)来促进实现，从而在保留全文信息的同时提升计算效率。

Result: 在两个模型和数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，与六种基线方法相比，TSPORec在推荐性能上最高提升31.25%，在计算效率上最高提升63.4%。

Conclusion: TSPORec证明了通过精准的token选择可以同时实现推荐性能提升与计算效率优化，为LLM-based推荐系统在成本和效果之间找到了更好的平衡点。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for improving recommendation systems. The effectiveness of LLMs arises from their ability to harness rich textual information and their capacity to model heterogeneous user preferences based on users' interaction history. However, due to the large-scale and deep architectures, LLM-based sequential recommendation approaches generally incur high inference costs, resulting in a low return on investment. To mitigate this cost, many existing approaches resort to using only the first few tokens of item descriptions, which inadvertently discards valuable information contained in the full text, thereby leading to suboptimal recommendation performance. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Token Selection approach for Preference Optimization in LLM-based sequential Recommendation, i.e., TSPORec, which accurately pinpoints informative tokens throughout the entire textual content to improve recommendation performance. Specifically, we design a three-stage pipeline to select informative tokens and introduce a novel proxy reward to facilitate the implementation. TSPORec not only enhances recommendation performance but also improves computational efficiency. Extensive experiments across two models and datasets demonstrate the superb performance (up to 31.25%) and efficiency (up to 63.4%) of our approach compared with six baseline approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/WNQzhu/TSPORec.git.

</details>


### [18] [IntHQ: Task-Interactive Hierarchical Query on Dual-Stream Representations for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09634)
*Junjie Sun,Longfei Xu,Huimin Yan,Wei Luo,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IntHQ，一个面向多任务生成式推荐系统的新型框架。针对现有方法中"从单一任务无关表征提取特征并接入预定义转化漏斗"方案存在的三重坍缩问题（源坍缩、关系坍缩、层级坍缩），IntHQ通过双流解耦（DSD）、任务交互建模（TIM）和层级查询（HQ）三个组件分别缓解。离线评测中超越多个编码器骨干，在线部署于高德地图为超亿用户服务，UVCTR相对提升1.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习在异构数据上对现代推荐系统至关重要，生成式模型正成为下一代推荐系统的支柱，但多任务学习与生成范式的结合仍未被充分探索。现有方案从单一任务无关表征提取特征，将任务接入预定义转化漏斗，本质上容易遭遇三重坍缩，限制了多任务生成式推荐的效果。

Method: 提出IntHQ框架，包含三个组件：(1)双流解耦（DSD）：早期注入任务身份到计算流，将共享上下文流与任务特定流分离，缓解信号稀释；(2)任务交互建模（TIM）：用显式跨任务交互替代预定义漏斗，让每个任务基于其前驱任务的已实现结果进行条件建模，且交互强度可学习、随输入自适应；(3)层级查询（HQ）：让每个任务在不同训练阶段跨不同层聚合多尺度信息。

Result: 离线评测中，IntHQ在四种代表性任务头配置下持续优于竞争性编码器骨干；在真实生产环境（高德地图旅行推荐，服务数亿用户）中部署，UVCTR相对提升1.60%。

Conclusion: IntHQ通过系统性地解决三重坍缩问题，成功将多任务学习有效集成到生成式推荐范式之中，并在大规模工业场景中验证了其有效性，为生成式多任务推荐提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Multi-task learning over heterogeneous data is fundamental to modern recommendation, while generative models are emerging as the backbone of next-generation recommenders. However, the integration of multi-task learning into the generative paradigm remains largely unexplored. Existing multi-task recommenders, in both discriminative and generative paradigms, extract task-relevant features from a single task-agnostic representation and wire tasks into a predefined conversion funnel. We show that this scheme is inherently prone to a threefold collapse. Source collapse, where task-specific signals are injected late and diluted in the shared latent space. Relational collapse, where task dependencies are either implicitly absorbed by the backbone or statically fixed by predefined funnels. Hierarchical collapse, where tasks depend on features at different scales and shift across training stages. We propose IntHQ, a multi-task generative recommender with three components, each alleviating one collapse. Dual-Stream Decoupling (DSD) injects task identity into computation stream early and separates the shared context stream from the task-specific stream, alleviating signal dilution. Task-Interactive Modeling (TIM) replaces the predefined funnel with explicit cross-task interaction, letting each task condition on the realized outcomes of its predecessors with learned, input-adaptive strength. Hierarchical Querying (HQ) lets each task gather multi-scale information across different layers at different training stages. In offline evaluations, IntHQ consistently outperforms competitive encoder backbones under four representative task-head configurations. Deployed in production on Amap, serving hundreds of millions of users for travel recommendation, IntHQ yields a 1.60\% relative UVCTR lift.

</details>


### [19] [Listwise Cross-Encoder Fine-Tuning vs. Agentic Instruction Tuning for LLM Rerankers: A Systematic Study in Medical Procedure Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09650)
*Matan Fainzilber,Shlomit Plavner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了医疗程序重排序的两种范式：微调的小型交叉编码器（如109M参数的MedCPT）与4B参数的大语言模型重排序器（Qwen3-Reranker-4B）。结果显示，在NDCG@3上小型交叉编码器以37倍少的参数反而高出2.6个百分点，在Spearman相关性上高出13.3个百分点，并提供了可扩展的数据集构建流程和生产部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗保险信息检索中，将医疗程序与患者查询进行重排序是关键任务，但患者语言与临床术语之间存在巨大的词汇鸿沟，导致检索效果不佳。作者希望系统性地比较不同重排序范式在该生产任务上的表现。

Method: 1) 构建专门的2,647条查询、708个保险服务数据集；2) 范式一：使用ListNet等listwise学习排序目标微调小型交叉编码器（MedCPT、MiniLM-L12），并测试不同层冻结配置；3) 范式二：使用4B参数的Qwen3-Reranker-4B指令重排序器，通过GPT-4.1驱动的代理优化循环迭代改进提示词；4) 提出基于LLM的可扩展数据集构建管线。

Result: 109M参数的交叉编码器（ListNet微调）在NDCG@3上比4B参数模型高出2.6个百分点，在Spearman相关性上高出13.3个百分点，且参数量仅为后者的1/37。

Conclusion: 在医疗程序重排序的生产场景中，小型交叉编码器在效率和效果上均优于大型指令重排序器，并提供了代码和样例数据集以支持可复现性和跨领域适配。

Abstract: Reranking medical procedures against patient queries is a critical component of health insurance information retrieval, complicated by a substantial lexical gap between patient language and clinical nomenclature. We present a systematic comparison of two reranking paradigms for this production task: (1) small cross-encoders (MedCPT, MiniLM-L12) fine-tuned with listwise learning-to-rank objectives across layer freezing configurations, and (2) Qwen3-Reranker-4B, a 4B-parameter instruction reranker whose prompt is iteratively refined via an agentic optimization loop driven by GPT-4.1. On a purpose-built dataset of 2,647 queries across 708 insurance services, we find that a 109M-parameter cross-encoder fine-tuned with ListNet outperforms the 4B-parameter model by 2.6 percentage points on NDCG@3 and 13.3 points on Spearman correlation - at 37x fewer parameters. We report practical findings, a scalable LLM based dataset construction pipeline, and deployment trade-offs relevant to production reranking systems. We release our code and a sample dataset to support reproducibility and adaptation to other domains.

</details>

### [20] [World Simulator: Queer Erotica and the Absurdity of AI Video Models That Promise the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07510)
*Adam Cole,Mick Grierson*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一件名为"World Simulator"的视频装置艺术作品，该作品将男性同性色情内容输入AI视频到视频的生成管道中，由于训练数据的过滤和缺失，系统无法识别这些图像，反而幻觉出荒诞的替代场景（如厨房电器、奇异建筑、抽象肉体）。作品通过可视化AI的认知盲区，批判了AI视频模型自封"世界模拟器"的狂妄主张，并进一步质疑模拟本身的价值，探讨更具生命肯定性的感官表征形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视频模型被市场营销为"世界模拟器"，声称能建模无限现实，但这些模型系统性地排除了具身体验的重要方面，尤其是性。这种声称与实际的认知盲区之间存在巨大鸿沟，需要被揭示和批判。

Method: 创作一件视频装置艺术作品，将明确的男同性恋色情内容输入AI视频到视频（video-to-video）生成管道，观察系统如何处理其训练数据中缺失的图像类型，从而暴露模型的幻觉模式及其结构性盲区。

Result: 系统因缺乏相关训练数据而无法识别这些图像，产生幻觉性的替代场景，将亲密行为转化为厨房电器、奇异建筑和抽象肉体等平庸场景，可视化了合成知识的局限。

Conclusion: 该作品挑战了"世界模拟器"标签的傲慢，指出一个主要基于大规模过滤视频数据集训练的系统，在结构上对身体视而不见的情况下，无法声称模拟整个世界。同时进一步质疑模拟本身的价值，探索更广阔、更具生命肯定性的感官表征可能性。

Abstract: Increasingly, AI video models are marketed as "world simulators," suggesting their ability to model infinite realities. Despite such claims, these models systematically exclude significant aspects of embodied human experience, particularly sexuality. World Simulator is a video installation exploring the poetic friction between these universal claims and the models' inherent blindness. To do so, the work feeds explicit gay erotica into an AI video-to-video pipeline. Lacking the training data to recognize these images, the system hallucinates surreal alternatives, transforming intimate acts into banal scenes of kitchen appliances, strange architectures, and abstract flesh. By visualizing the limits of synthetic knowledge, the work challenges the hubris of the "world simulator" label, asking how a system, trained primarily on large filtered video datasets, can claim to simulate the world while remaining structurally blind to the body. Beyond this critique, we question the value of simulation itself, asking what forms of sensual representation might offer more expansive, life-affirming possibilities.

</details>


### [21] [SAMOT: State-Aware Step Modulation and Optimal Transport Matching for Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08147)
*Kai Peng,Yunzhe Shen,Miao Zhang,Leiye Liu,Wei Ji,Jingjing Li,Yongri Piao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向音视频实例分割（AVIS）的新框架，通过自适应动态步长调制（ADSM）和基于最优传输的匹配调制（OT-MM）来解决模态状态变化和跨模态分布差异两大挑战，在AVIS基准上取得了最先进的性能（FSLA +3.76, HOTA +2.75, mAP +2.58）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVIS任务涉及长视频序列中的实例级建模，面临两大挑战：(1) 复杂的模态状态变化破坏了长程建模能力；(2) 音视频模态间存在显著的结构和分布差异，阻碍了精确的实例级关联。现有基于固定步长Transformer和递归Mamba的方法无法适应模态状态变化，而隐式匹配方法忽略了固有的分布不一致性问题。

Method: 提出ADSM和OT-MM两个核心模块：ADSM利用时间变化、跨模态差异和历史上下文自适应调制Mamba步长，在快速响应模态状态变化与稳定长程建模之间取得平衡；OT-MM将实例级跨模态匹配显式建模为带熵正则化的最优传输问题，通过对数域Sinkhorn迭代求解，并引入MMD正则化增强分布级一致性。

Result: 在AVIS基准上取得最先进性能，FSLA提升+3.76，HOTA提升+2.75，mAP提升+2.58，并通过全面的定性可视化验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应步长调制和显式最优传输匹配能有效解决AVIS中的模态状态变化和分布差异问题，验证了该方法在长视频音视频实例分割任务上的优越性，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation (AVIS) aims to simultaneously classify, segment, and track sounding objects within video sequences. Unlike Audio-Visual Semantic Segmentation (AVS), AVIS involves instance-level modeling across longer video sequences, introducing two key challenges: (1) complex modality-state changes disrupt long-range modeling, and (2) substantial structural and distributional discrepancies between modalities hinder precise instance-level association. Existing methods rely on fixed-step Transformers and recursive Mamba models, lacking adaptability to modality-state changes. In addition, methods performing implicit matching ignore the inherent distributional inconsistencies. To address these issues, we propose a framework with Adaptive Dynamic Step Modulation (ADSM) and Optimal Transport-based Matching Modulation (OT-MM). ADSM adaptively modulates Mamba step sizes using temporal variation, cross-modal discrepancy, and historical context, balancing rapid response to modality-state changes with stable long-range modeling. OT-MM explicitly formulates instance-level cross-modal matching as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem solved via log-domain Sinkhorn iterations, and further enforces distribution-level coherence with an MMD regularizer. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the AVIS benchmark (+3.76 FSLA, +2.75 HOTA, +2.58 mAP), verified through comprehensive qualitative visualizations. The code and model are available at https://github.com/happylife-pk/SAMOT.

</details>

### [22] [Space-Time Information Interchangeability in Dynamical Systems: Conditions and Bounds for Replacing Spatial Sensors with Temporal Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07728)
*Maryam Reza,Farbod Faraji*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个理论框架，比较时间测量历史与指定瞬时空间传感器阵列的信息内容，通过先验白化信息算子表示两者，并提出一个通用的时空信息交换定理，给出在每一状态方向保留规定比例空间参考信息的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力学状态重构问题需要从少数位置的测量中推断高维空间状态。由于动力学在空间和时间上耦合演化，时间测量可能包含传感器位置之外的状态分量信息。需要理论框架来回答时间测量能否以及如何替代空间传感器覆盖的问题。

Method: 将时间历史与空间参考阵列用先验白化信息算子表示，考虑先验变异性、传感器几何和测量噪声，同时保留不确定性减少的方向分布。推导通用时空信息交换定理，给出充要条件。针对压缩型、谱分离酉型和同时可对角化耗散线性动力学以及高斯噪声下的随机线性估计导出显式历史长度界；对于非线性系统，结合全局延迟映射单射性与均匀局部Fisher信息条件。

Result: 回答了三个实际问题：所需时间测量的数量和总信息量；规定比例是否可达到或存在信息上限；可达到时所需的历史长度。数值实验测试了方向性交换长度、信息上限和有限记忆界，并评估了非线性延迟映射可区分性和局部Fisher信息预测。

Conclusion: 该框架为确定时间测量何时可以减少全状态重构的空间传感器覆盖提供了明确基础。

Abstract: Many dynamical-state reconstruction problems seek to infer a high-dimensional spatial state from measurements at only a few locations. Because the governing dynamics couple evolution across space and time, temporal measurements can contain information about state components beyond the sensor locations. This work develops a theoretical framework comparing the information content of a temporal measurement history with that of a specified instantaneous spatial sensor array. The temporal history and spatial reference array are represented by prior-whitened information operators that account for prior variability, sensor geometry, and measurement noise while preserving the directional distribution of uncertainty reduction. A general space-time information-exchange theorem gives necessary and sufficient conditions for retaining a prescribed fraction of the spatial-reference information in every state direction. It answers three practical questions. First, how many temporal measurements and how much total information are required? Second, is the prescribed fraction attainable, or does a limiting information ceiling rule out every finite history? Third, when attainable, what history length is sufficient? Explicit history-length bounds are derived for contractive, spectrally separated unitary, and simultaneously diagonalizable dissipative linear dynamics, and for stochastic linear estimation under Gaussian process and measurement noise. For nonlinear systems, global delay-map injectivity is combined with a uniform local Fisher-information condition. Numerical experiments test directional exchange lengths, information ceilings, and finite-memory bounds, and assess nonlinear delay-map distinguishability and local Fisher-information predictions. The framework provides an explicit basis for determining when temporal measurements can reduce spatial sensor coverage for full-state reconstruction.

</details>


### [23] [Semantic-Aided Iterative Decoding for Uplink Non-Orthogonal Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07897)
*Wenjing Wei,Chentao Yue,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向上行非正交传输的语义辅助迭代译码（Sem-IR）方案，利用共享自然语言源的结构先验来增强NOMA接收机的迭代干扰消除性能。在基站端，ESE与K个并行LDPC译码器迭代消除用户间干扰，通过微调的ByT5字节级语言模型利用已收敛用户形成的前缀预测未收敛用户的字节后验，并将其边缘化为比特级LLR与LDPC后验凸组合，实现语言模型与物理层迭代的闭环反馈。仿真表明Sem-IR在BLER上优于TDMA和无语义反馈的NOMA，在8 dB时相比NOMA有数量级的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NOMA接收机依赖物理层迭代进行干扰消除，但未利用源信号（自然语言）的语义结构。自然语言具有高度结构性和可预测性，若能利用语言模型提供的先验信息辅助物理层译码，可显著提升非正交传输的可靠性，尤其是在高功率用户先收敛后可形成可靠语言前缀的条件下。

Method: 1) 多用户将共享句子的不同片段进行LDPC编码后叠加传输至基站；2) 基站使用迭代基本信号估计器（ESE）与K个并行LDPC译码器进行迭代干扰消除；3) 高功率用户通过奇偶校验和语言合理性检查后，其译码字节形成可靠语言前缀；4) 微调的ByT5字节级语言模型利用该前缀预测未收敛用户的字节后验分布；5) 字节后验被边缘化为比特级LLR，并与LDPC后验在迭代循环内进行凸组合，形成闭环语义反馈。

Result: 仿真结果表明，Sem-IR在块错误率（BLER）上优于正交时分多址（TDMA）和相同但不含语义反馈的NOMA接收机，在8 dB信噪比下相比无语义反馈的NOMA实现了数量级的BLER降低。

Conclusion: 将语言模型语义先验与物理层迭代译码结合，能够有效利用自然语言的统计结构提升NOMA上行传输的可靠性，验证了语义通信与非正交多址融合的可行性与显著增益。

Abstract: This paper proposes semantic-aided iterative decoding (Sem-IR) for uplink non-orthogonal transmission of a shared natural-language source. K users each hold one segment of a common sentence and superimpose low-density parity-check (LDPC) coded transmissions over an additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel. At the base station, an iterative elementary signal estimator (ESE) and K parallel LDPC decoders progressively cancel inter-user interference. As high-power users pass both parity and language-plausibility checks earlier, their decoded bytes form a reliable linguistic prefix for the remaining users; a fine-tuned ByT5 byte-level language model exploits this prefix to predict byte posteriors for the unconverged user. The byte posteriors are marginalized to bit-level log-likelihood ratios and convex-combined with the LDPC posteriors inside the iterative loop. The resulting feedback closes the loop between the language model and the physical-layer iteration. Simulations show that Sem-IR outperforms orthogonal time-division access (TDMA) and the same NOMA receiver without semantic feedback in block error rate (BLER), yielding an order-of-magnitude reduction over NOMA at 8 dB.

</details>


### [24] [Error characterization and error correction approaches in combinatorial DNA-based storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07991)
*Inbal Preuss,Omer Sabary,Ryan Gabrys,Zohar Yakhini,Eitan Yaakobi,Leon Anavy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了组合DNA存储系统中的不对称组合擦除错误，提出了一种基于张量积码的不对称纠错编码方法，通过将标准纠错码（如RS码）与不对称Varshamov-Tenengolts码结合，在低测序深度场景下显著优于传统的2D RS方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合DNA编码通过短mer组合提高逻辑密度，但引入了独特的合成和测序错误，特别是不对称组合擦除错误（组合字母集合中丢失单个短mer）。这种错误在大规模系统和低测序覆盖度下非常普遍，但现有纠错方案未针对其不对称特性进行优化。

Method: 1) 首先对两个已发表的组合DNA数据集进行表征，揭示不对称擦除错误的高频发生。2) 进行大规模实验验证，确认测序深度低于50 reads/sequence时擦除错误频率急剧增加。3) 开发了基于张量积码的不对称纠错码，将标准擦除和替换纠错码（如Reed-Solomon码）与不对称Varshamov-Tenengolts码集成。4) 通过模拟和第二个大规模实验与2D RS方案进行对比验证。

Result: 该方法在擦除错误主导的场景中持续优于2D RS方案，在低测序覆盖度下展现出优越的解码准确性，而2D RS在此条件下难以成功解码数据。

Conclusion: 研究证明了针对组合DNA中错误不对称特性定制的纠错方案的重要性，为组合DNA存储系统提供了更可靠的纠错框架。

Abstract: Data storage in DNA has recently emerged as a promising archival solution, offering space-efficient and long-lasting digital storage. Combinatorial DNA encoding enhances this potential by increasing the logical density through combinations of DNA shortmers, where each sequence position is represented by a set of predefined short DNA fragments, allowing more data to be encoded using fewer synthesis cycles. However, this method introduces unique synthesis and sequencing errors. In this study, we characterize errors in combinatorial DNA-based storage systems. We reveal that asymmetric combinatorial erasure errors, defined as the omission of a single shortmer from the set defining the combinatorial letter, are a prevalent error type, particularly in large-scale systems where read coverage is limited. In two previously published datasets, we observed a high frequency of erasure errors, where missing sequences obstruct the reconstruction of combinatorial letters. We conducted a large-scale experimental proof-of-concept and confirmed that erasure errors become increasingly prominent with reduced sequencing depth: below 50 reads per sequence, their frequency sharply increased. We developed an asymmetric error-correcting code for these errors, utilizing tensor-product codes to integrate standard erasure and substitution-correcting codes (such as Reed-Solomon (RS) codes) with asymmetric Varshamov-Tenengolts codes. We validated its performance in simulations and in a second large-scale experiment directly comparing it with the more straightforward 2D RS scheme. Our method consistently outperformed 2D RS, particularly in erasure-dominated scenarios, and demonstrated superior decoding accuracy under low coverage conditions where 2D RS struggled to decode the data. Our findings demonstrate the importance of error correction schemes tailored to the asymmetric nature of errors in combinatorial DNA.

</details>


### [25] [More on Codes for Combinatorial Composite DNA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08018)
*Zuo Ye,Omer Sabary,Ryan Gabrys,Eitan Yaakobi,Ohad Elishco*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了$(t,e)$-复合非对称纠错码（$(t,e)$-CAECCs）的唯一可译码与列表可译码构造问题。针对一般参数$m,n,w,t,e$，提出了冗余至多为$(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$的新构造，其中$(2,e)$-CAECCs在冗余意义上是最优的。当$m$为素数幂时冗余可进一步降至$(t-1)\log(m)-O(\log(m))$。为扩大码字规模引入弱$B_e$-集这一组合对象；当$e=w$时给出高效编解码方案。最后探讨列表译码的增益，证明了列表大小为$t!$或$t$的指数函数时存在常数冗余的列表可译码$(t,e)$-CAECCs，列表大小为2时存在冗余为$\log(m)+O(1)$的列表可译码$(3,2)$-CAECCs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合非对称错误模型近年来被提出，它描述的是$m\times n$二进制矩阵中至多$t$行发生错误、且每行至多$e$个$1\to 0$错误的场景。该模型在存储系统（如闪存、DNA存储）中有实际应用背景，但此前缺乏系统性的编码构造研究，尤其是高效冗余的构造方案。填补这一空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 本文采用组合构造与代数方法相结合的方式：(1) 针对一般参数提出冗余为$(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$的CAECC构造；(2) 当$m$为素数幂时利用代数方法进一步降低冗余；(3) 引入弱$B_e$-集这一新组合对象以扩大码字规模；(4) 利用$e=w$的特殊情形设计高效编解码算法；(5) 通过放宽唯一译码为列表译码来获得冗余降低的构造。

Result: 提出了冗余至多$(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$的一般构造，其中$(2,e)$-CAECCs在冗余意义上达到最优。当$m$为素数幂时冗余降至$(t-1)\log(m)-O(\log(m))$。列表译码方面，列表大小为$t!$或$t$的指数函数时可实现常数冗余；列表大小为2时，(3,2)-CAECCs的冗余为$\log(m)+O(1)$。

Conclusion: 本文系统地推进了$(t,e)$-CAECCs的构造理论，在唯一译码和列表译码两个方向都取得了显著进展：既给出了部分最优的唯一可译码构造，又证明了列表译码可以大幅降低冗余需求，为复合非对称错误模型的实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we focus on constructions of unique-decodable/list-decodable on the recently studied $(t,e)$-composite-asymmetric error-correcting codes ($(t,e)$-CAECCs). Let $X$ be an $m\times n$ binary matrix, in which each row has Hamming weight $w$. When at most $t$ rows of $X$ suffer from errors and in each of these erroneous rows, there are at most $e$ $1 \to 0$ errors, we say that a $(t,e)$-composite-asymmetric-error occurs in $X$. For general $m,n,w,t,e$, we propose new constructions of $(t,e)$-CAECCs with redundancy at most $(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$, where $O(1)$ is a number independent of the code-length $m$. In particular, this gives a class of $(2,e)$-CAECCs that are optimal in terms of their redundancy. %(in terms of redundancy, regarded as a function of the number of rows $m$) s. When $m$ is a prime power, the redundancy can be further reduced to $(t-1)\log(m)-O(\log(m))$. To further increase the size of these codes, we introduce a combinatorial object called a weak $B_e$-sets. When $e=w$, we show an efficient way to encode/decode our codes. At last, we investigate how much we can gain if we relax the requirement of uniquely decoding to list-decoding. It is shown that when the list size is $t!$ or an exponential function of $t$, there are list-decodable $(t,e)$-CAECCs with constant redundancy. When the list size is two, we show that there are list-decodable $(3,2)$-CAECCs with redundancy $\log(m)+O(1)$.

</details>

*Rostislav Matveev*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文进一步发展了随机变量对形状函数理论，证明当(X,Y)均匀支持于双正则二分图的边上时，形状函数值等于图关联矩阵在特定Lebesgue指数下的算子范数的对数，并建立了形状函数满足的凸性、单调性、组合不等式及条件化行为等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状函数是在arXiv:2606.23849中引入的，用于推导随机变量对纠缠(可提取互信息量)的谱界。本文旨在进一步发展形状函数的理论，特别是建立其与图论中算子范数的联系，从而实现数值近似，并系统建立其基本性质。

Method: 作者证明了双正则二分图上均匀支持的随机变量对的形状函数等于该图关联矩阵在由(α,β)确定的Lebesgue指数下的算子范数的对数。利用这一识别关系，通过数值方法可近似计算形状函数，并通过对偶关系得到扩展剖面(张力区域)。此外，作者建立了形状函数的凸性、单调性、组合不等式、条件化行为及变量拼接等性质。

Result: 建立了形状函数与双正则二分图关联矩阵算子范数之间的精确对应关系，实现了形状函数及张力区域的数值近似，并系统建立了形状函数的多个数学性质(凸性、单调性、组合不等式等)。

Conclusion: 该工作将信息论中的形状函数与图论中的谱理论联系起来，为形状函数的计算提供了有效途径，同时丰富了对形状函数基本性质的理解，为进一步应用于纠缠谱界等问题奠定基础。

Abstract: The shape function of a pair of finite-valued random variables was introduced in arXiv:2606.23849, where it was used to derive a spectral bound on the entanglement of the pair, a quantity measuring the extent to which their mutual information can be extracted. In this article, we further develop the theory of shape functions for pairs of random variables. We prove that, when $(X,Y)$ is uniformly supported on the edges of a biregular bipartite graph, the value of the shape function $\mathcal{S}(X,Y)(α,β)$ equals the logarithm of the operator norm of the graph's incidence matrix with respect to Lebesgue exponents determined by $(α,β)$.This identification, in particular, enables the numerical approximation of the shape function and, by duality, of the extension profile, also known as the tension region, of the pair. We also establish a collection of relations and inequalities satisfied by shape functions, including convexity and monotonicity properties, composition inequalities, and relations describing their behavior under conditioning and the adjoining of variables.

</details>
