*Mahdi Mahvari,Gerhard Kramer,Shlomo Shamai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对带限加性高斯白噪声信道中电平受限输入（一位量化信号）的容量下界进行了推导。通过引入概率时间整形（PTS）技术，作者推导了最优整形密度，并证明在高信噪比下，相比现有最佳下界可提升至少1.94 dB；同时提出一种更简单的序列PTS方案，在高SNR下可获得1.64 dB的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一比特量化信号（电平受限输入）在带限连续时间信道中的容量下界研究具有重要意义。现有下界不够紧致，需要新的方法来提升容量下界，从而更好地理解一比特量化系统的理论性能极限。

Method: 采用概率时间整形（PTS）技术，对每个奈奎斯特采样间隔内具有一个数据相关符号翻转的信号构建整形密度分布，推导满足可逆性约束的最优整形密度，并评估信道容量下界。同时提出一个简化的序列PTS方案作为低复杂度替代。

Result: 计算表明，在高信噪比下，PTS方法可将现有最佳容量下界提升至少1.94 dB；简化的序列PTS方案也可获得1.64 dB的高SNR增益。

Conclusion: 概率时间整形方法能有效提升一比特量化带限AWGN信道的容量下界，性能增益显著，且简化的序列方案在降低复杂度同时仍能获得大部分增益。

Abstract: Level-constraints model one-bit-quantized signaling over real-alphabet continuous-time channels. New lower bounds on the capacity of bandlimited, additive white Gaussian noise channels with level-constrained inputs are derived by using probabilistic temporal shaping (PTS). The optimal shaping density is derived for signals with one data-dependent sign change per Nyquist-rate sample, which ensures they satisfy an invertibility requirement. Calculations show that PTS improves the best existing lower bound by at least 1.94 dB at high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). A simpler sequential PTS scheme achieves a gain of 1.64 dB at high SNR.

</details>


### [28] [Dependency-Aware Reliability Allocation for Open-Vocabulary Scene-Graph Semantic Packets over Latency- and Energy-Constrained Wireless Visual Uplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08110)
*Yuli Liu,Jiacheng Ruan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无线视觉感知上行链路中开放词汇场景图的依赖感知可靠性分配问题。由于语义三元组包和其前置词汇增量包之间存在依赖关系，论文提出了一种联合选择调制编码方案、发射功率和最大HARQ深度的优化方法，利用多仿射失真结构的语义依赖拉格朗日块方法实现精确求解，在30个实例中28个达到全局最优，性能优于多种现有HARQ和UEP方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线视觉感知上行链路日益传输结构化语义包而非原始图像或特征张量。在开放词汇场景图中，索引化的主体-关系-客体三元组只有在自身数据包和所有前置词汇增量包都被恢复时才可解释。这种依赖结构无法被传统的丢包模型、层优先级或语义特征HARQ规则所捕捉，因此需要专门研究依赖感知的可靠性分配策略。

Method: 将数据包分为词汇增量包和索引三元组包，构建前置依赖图；推导Chase合并HARQ的残余失败率、反馈感知的时延和能量、以及依赖感知的语义失真；将问题建模为有限离散问题，联合优化每个包的调制编码方案、发射功率和最大HARQ深度；提出语义依赖拉格朗日块方法，利用多仿射失真结构对每个包更新执行精确有限候选最小化。

Result: 在30个缩减菜单实例的穷举审计中，28个达到全局最优，平均相对差距0.052%，最差1.10%。解析链路预算扫描、公开场景图轨迹、多会话多种子验证和表驱动BLER测试均表明，该方法在词汇诱导语义失败率和关键查询成功率上优于依赖无关HARQ、语义优先级HARQ、优先级UEP、仅UEP和均匀HARQ。

Conclusion: 依赖感知的可靠性分配对于语义通信至关重要。词汇包具有共享的下游启用价值，取决于其他前置包和三元组包的当前可靠性，这与固定优先级UEP本质不同。所提出的方法在期望帧时延和能量约束下实现了高质量的系统性能，为开放词汇场景图语义通信提供了有效的可靠性分配方案。

Abstract: Wireless visual perception uplinks increasingly transmit structured semantic packets rather than raw images or opaque feature tensors. In an open-vocabulary scene graph, an indexed subject-relation-object triplet is interpretable only if its own packet and all prerequisite vocabulary-increment packets are recovered, creating a dependency structure not captured by conventional packet-loss, layer-priority, or semantic-feature HARQ rules. We study dependency-aware reliability allocation for such packets under expected frame-delay and frame-energy constraints. The model separates vocabulary-increment and indexed-triplet packets, builds their prerequisite graph, and derives Chase-combining HARQ residual failure, feedback-aware delay and energy, and dependency-aware semantic distortion. The resulting finite discrete problem jointly selects each packet's modulation and coding scheme, transmit power, and maximum HARQ depth. Unlike fixed-priority UEP, a vocabulary packet receives a shared downstream enabling value that depends on the current reliabilities of other prerequisite and triplet packets. Exploiting the resulting multi-affine distortion structure, the proposed semantic-dependency Lagrangian block method performs exact finite-candidate minimization for each packet update. In a small-scale exhaustive audit over 30 reduced-menu instances, the method reaches the global optimum in 28 cases, with mean and worst observed relative gaps of 0.052% and 1.10%. Analytical link-budget sweeps, public scene-graph traces, multi-session and multi-seed validation, and table-driven BLER tests show lower vocabulary-induced semantic failure and higher critical-query success than dependency-agnostic HARQ, semantic-priority HARQ, priority UEP, UEP-only, and uniform HARQ.

</details>


### [29] [Optimal Exponent of the Single-Error Correction Threshold with Fixed Redundancy for Analog Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08171)
*Zhengyi Jiang,Wenhao Liu,Zhongyi Huang,Hanxu Hou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了模拟纠错码（Analog ECCs）单错误纠错阈值Γ₂(C)的下界：对于固定冗余r≥2的实数[n,k]线性码，Γ₂(C) ≥ (a_r/(√r·β_{r-1}·2^{1/(r-1)}))·n^{1+1/(r-1)}，并进一步证明了Γ₂(C) ≥ n^{1+1/(r-1)}/(4π√3·r)。这一结果与[2]中的上界相匹配，确认了指数n^{1+1/(r-1)}的最优性，完成了Analog ECCs单错误纠错阈值的渐近刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟纠错码（Analog ECCs）用于解决存内计算中向量-矩阵乘法因模拟噪声和稀疏异常值引起的错误。Roth[1]提出了一个基本开放问题：固定冗余r≥2时，实数[n,k]线性码的单错误纠错阈值Γ₂(C)的下界。Li等人[2]证明了r=2时下界为csc²(π/2n)，且对固定r≥2存在上界O(n^{1+1/(r-1)})，但缺乏匹配的下界，这是本文要解决的核心开放问题。

Method: 本文通过构造性的几何与分析论证，为固定冗余r≥2的任意[n,k]实数线性码建立了Γ₂(C)的一般下界。关键步骤包括利用单位球面S^d的表面积、Gamma函数等几何常数（a_r和β_d）来量化错误校正阈值，并通过较简洁的估计进一步导出Γ₂(C) ≥ n^{1+1/(r-1)}/(4π√3·r)的具体下界。

Result: 本文证明了对于任意冗余r（2≤r<n）的实数[n,k]线性码，Γ₂(C) ≥ (a_r/(√r·β_{r-1}·2^{1/(r-1)}))·n^{1+1/(r-1)}，并简化为Γ₂(C) ≥ n^{1+1/(r-1)}/(4π√3·r)。这与[2]中的上界O(n^{1+1/(r-1)})匹配，首次从理论上证明了n^{1+1/(r-1)}是固定冗余条件下单错误纠错阈值的最优指数。

Conclusion: 本文解决了[2]中遗留的下界匹配问题，完成了对Analog ECCs单错误纠错阈值Γ₂(C)的渐近完整刻画。研究表明，在存内计算场景中，单错误纠错阈值随码长n的增长具有n^{1+1/(r-1)}的最优渐近行为，为设计高效模拟纠错码提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Analog error-correcting codes (Analog ECCs), introduced by Roth [1], address errors in vector-matrix multiplication arising from analog noise and sparse outliers in in-memory computing. A fundamental open problem concerns the lower bound on the single-error correction threshold $Γ_2(\mathcal C)$ for real $[n,k]$ linear codes with fixed redundancy $r=n-k\geq 2$. Li et al. [2] recently established that for redundancy $r=2$, every real $[n,n-2]$ linear code $\mathcal{C}$ satisfies $Γ_2(\mathcal C)\geq \csc^2(\fracπ{2n})$, resolving an open problem in [1], and showed that, for every fixed $r \geq 2$, there exists a class of $[n,k]$ linear code $\mathcal{C}$ over $\mathbb{R}$ such that $Γ_2(\mathcal{C}) \leq O(n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}})$.
  This paper proves the matching converse in [2]. For every $[n,k]$ linear code $\mathcal{C}\subseteq \mathbb R^n$ with fixed redundancy $2\leq r<n$, we show that \[ Γ_2(\mathcal C)\ge \frac{a_r}{\sqrt{r}\,β_{r-1}\,2^{\frac{1}{r-1}}}\cdot n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}}, \] where $a_r =\frac{Γ(\frac{r}{2})}{\sqrtπ\,Γ(\frac{r+1}{2})}$ and $β_d = \left(\frac{dπ^{d-1}|\mathbb S^d|}{|\mathbb S^{d-1}|}\right)^{\frac{1}{d}}$ for positive integer $d$. Here $\mathbb S^d$ denotes the unit sphere in $\mathbb R^{d+1}$, $|\mathbb S^d|$ its surface area, and $Γ(\cdot)$ the Gamma function. In particular, we further show that $Γ_2(\mathcal C)\geq \frac{1}{4π\sqrt{3}r}\cdot n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}}$. Together with the upper bound in [2], this confirms that the exponent $n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}}$ is optimal, completing the asymptotic characterization of the single-error correction threshold for Analog ECCs.

</details>


### [30] [A counterexample to the Etzion-Silberstein conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08478)
*Jitendra Prajapati*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Etzion-Silberstein猜想的一个反例：对于列高为(5,5,5,5,1,1)的Ferrers图，最小秩距离为3时，二进制线性码的最大维数为11而非Singleton型上界所给出的12，从而否证了该猜想。作者还通过迭代行锥传播恒等式，在任意最小秩距离d≥3下都构造了二进制反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Etzion-Silberstein猜想是Ferrers图秩度量码领域的一个核心开放问题，它断言Singleton型上界对于所有Ferrers图、最小秩距离和有限域都是可达的。验证或否证该猜想对于理解线性Ferrers图秩度量码的结构和极限至关重要。

Method: 作者采用反证法：假设存在维度为12的码，通过归约将其化归为三类等价的二进制[4×4,12,2] MRD码之一。利用秩分布参数消去两个等价类，对剩余的一类通过核轨道分析（4个核轨道）和精确提升系统（exact lift system）的不可满足性证明来排除所有可能性。同时，独立编写了验证器，包括对所有8,382,465个核进行无轨道约化的原始枚举。此外还证明了行锥传播恒等式并迭代使用以构造更多反例。

Result: 证明了在指定Ferrers图E上，最小秩距离3的二进制线性码最大维数精确为11（给出了显式构造），而非上界12，从而否证了Etzion-Silberstein猜想。进一步通过迭代行锥传播恒等式，对于所有最小秩距离d≥3都构造了界为12而最优值为11的二进制反例。

Conclusion: Etzion-Silberstein猜想在一般形式上是不成立的。作者不仅给出了首个反例，还通过行锥传播恒等式系统性地构造了无穷族反例，表明该猜想即使在二进制情况下也需要重新审视和修正。

Abstract: The Etzion-Silberstein conjecture asserts that the Singleton-type upper bound for linear Ferrers-diagram rank-metric codes is attained for every Ferrers diagram, minimum rank distance, and finite field. Let $E$ be the Ferrers diagram with column heights $(5,5,5,5,1,1)$. The bound for minimum rank distance $3$ is $12$. We prove that every binary linear code supported on $E$ with minimum rank distance $3$ has dimension at most $11$, and we give an explicit code of dimension $11$. Thus the optimum is exactly $11$, disproving the conjecture. The nonexistence proof reduces a hypothetical dimension-$12$ code to one of the three equivalence classes of binary $[4\times 4,12,2]$ MRD codes. A rank-distribution argument eliminates two classes and leaves four kernel orbits in the field class; all four exact lift systems are unsatisfiable. Independently written verifiers reproduce the result, including a raw enumeration of all $8,382,465$ kernels without orbit reduction. We also prove an exact row-cone propagation identity. Iterating it produces binary counterexamples with bound $12$ and optimum $11$ at every minimum rank distance $d \geq 3$.

</details>


### [31] [Memory AMP: Overflow Avoidance, Complexity Reduction, and Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08513)
*Shunqi Huang,Lei Liu,Brian M. Kurkoski*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对记忆近似消息传递（MAMP）算法族进行系统性改进：解决GD-MAMP在大条件数下的数值溢出问题，提出两种低复杂度变体（部分记忆和减少矩阵-向量乘积次数），并发展出统一的梯度基MAMP设计框架。该框架将WS-CG-VAMP作为特例，揭示了其有限精度不稳定的根源（灾难性抵消），并推导出更高效的等价形式WS-CG-VAMP(r)。实验表明GD-MAMP在小条件数下收敛更快，而WS-CG-VAMP(r)在大条件数下理论更快但在IEEE双精度下可能发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMP类算法在高维噪声线性系统中广泛用于信号恢复，MAMP框架引入了记忆项机制。尽管GD-MAMP已提出，但存在三个关键问题：（1）大条件数下中间变量超出浮点数范围导致溢出；（2）计算复杂度仍有优化空间；（3）缺乏统一的梯度基框架来理解不同MAMP算法的关系及数值稳定性。

Method: （1）解决GD-MAMP的溢出问题；（2）提出两个低复杂度变体：部分记忆替代全记忆、每次迭代矩阵-向量乘积从3次减少到2次；（3）建立通用的梯度基MAMP设计框架，该框架可推导出WS-CG-VAMP作为特例，并分析其正交化参数计算中的灾难性抵消问题；（4）推导等价重表述WS-CG-VAMP(r)，将矩阵-向量乘积最多减少50%。

Result: 低复杂度变体在收敛速度上无明显退化；WS-CG-VAMP的计算存在灾难性抵消导致有限精度不稳定；WS-CG-VAMP(r)显著减少计算量；以矩阵-向量乘积为度量，GD-MAMP在小条件数下收敛更快，而WS-CG-VAMP(r)在大条件数下在高精度算术中更快，但在IEEE双精度下可能因灾难性抵消而发散。

Conclusion: 本文通过解决数值溢出、提出低复杂度变体和建立统一梯度基框架，推进了MAMP算法的理论与实践。揭示WS-CG-VAMP的数值不稳定性根源，并提供了更高效且更稳定的替代方案，为在大动态范围条件数下选择合适算法提供了指导。

Abstract: Approximate message passing (AMP)-type algorithms are widely used for signal recovery in high-dimensional noisy linear systems. Recently, a framework called memory AMP (MAMP) was introduced, offering a new approach to incorporating memory terms within AMP algorithms. Building on this, a low-complexity gradient descent MAMP (GD-MAMP) was proposed for right-unitarily invariant matrices. In this paper, we first address an overflow problem in GD-MAMP caused by intermediate variables exceeding the floating-point range, which typically occurs when the condition number is large. Second, we propose two low-complexity variants of GD-MAMP: one replaces full-length memory with partial memory, while the other reduces the number of matrix-vector products per iteration by $1/3$ (from three to two). Neither degrades the convergence speed notably. Third, we develop a general gradient-based formulation for designing MAMP algorithms. This formulation recovers warm-started conjugate gradient VAMP (WS-CG-VAMP) as a special case. Furthermore, we show that the computation of the orthogonalization parameters in this formulation can suffer from catastrophic cancellation, which explains the finite-precision instability of WS-CG-VAMP. Finally, we derive an equivalent reformulation, termed WS-CG-VAMP(r), which reduces the number of matrix-vector products by up to $50\%$. Measured by matrix-vector products, GD-MAMP converges faster for small condition numbers, whereas WS-CG-VAMP(r) converges faster for large ones under high-precision arithmetic but may diverge in IEEE double precision due to catastrophic cancellation.

</details>


### [32] [Fluid Antenna System-Enabled Interference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08584)
*Jialu Zheng,Quanzhong Li,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了无信道状态信息条件下，流体天线系统（FAS）在多用户干扰信道中实现干扰对齐的方案。通过利用FAS提供的额外空间自由度来抵消有效干扰的实部分量，从而为期望信号检测保留无干扰空间，并推导了干扰对齐条件及相应的中断概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干扰对齐是提升多用户无线网络频谱效率的有效手段，但在发射端没有信道状态信息的场景下，干扰对齐的实现面临挑战。本文旨在探索利用流体天线系统提供的额外空间自由度来克服这一限制，实现无需发射端CSI的干扰对齐。

Method: 提出FAS增强的干扰对齐方案：利用流体天线系统提供的额外空间自由度，消除有效组合干扰的实部分量，使这些分量保留用于无干扰的期望信号检测。理论上推导了干扰对齐条件和相应的中断概率表达式。

Result: 仿真结果表明，理论推导的中断概率与数值结果吻合良好。此外，所提系统在平均和速率方面优于传统的非干扰信道单用户传输方案。

Conclusion: FAS增强的干扰对齐方案能够在发射端无CSI的情况下有效实现干扰对齐，不仅验证了理论推导的准确性，还在平均和速率性能上展现出优于传统单用户传输方案的潜力。

Abstract: Interference alignment is an efficient spectrum reuse scheme for multiuser wireless networks. In this letter, assuming no channel state information at the transmitters, we investigate a fluid antenna system (FAS)-enabled interference alignment scheme over a multiuser interference channel. In the proposed system, the additional spatial degrees of freedom provided by the FAS are exploited to nullify the real components of the effective combined interference such that those are reserved for interference-free desired signal detection. The interference alignment conditions and corresponding outage probabilities are theoretically derived. Simulations verify that the theoretically derived outage probabilities agree well with the numerical results. Furthermore, the proposed system is able to achieve a higher average sum rate than conventional single-user transmission schemes over non-interfering channels.

</details>


### [33] [Mean Gain Is Not a Guarantee: Risk-Controlled Open-Loop Scheduling for Ka-Band LEO Semantic Downlinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08635)
*Milad Bafarassat*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对低轨卫星图像下行链路调度问题，指出传统以平均性能为目标的调度器在单次过境场景下存在严重缺陷：即使平均多传1-2幅图像，仍有1/4到1/3的单独过境表现差于均匀功率方案。作者提出基于"后验失败率"来设定安全裕度，只保留在历史记录中极少出现反噬效应的调度设置，从而进行可证可靠的调度认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星过境时间仅几分钟且功率受限，需要通过天气统计提前规划功率分配。传统方法用安全裕度保证预报统计可靠，并以平均性能胜过均匀功率为标准。但作者发现对每次过境只有一次机会的场景，平均性能指标具有误导性——真正的目标应该是避免在个别过境中表现更差（即"反噬"风险）。

Method: 不再以统计可靠性或平均性能作为安全裕度的设定标准，而是在记录的历史过境数据上测量"如果采用该调度设置会发生多少次反噬"（即表现劣于均匀功率），只保留那些可证明反噬发生频率极低的设置。具体操作是：对学习到的编码器调度方案加以拒绝，而对具有更陡峭性能悬崖（sharper cliff）的编码器给出认证调度。

Result: 在真实Sentinel-2影像上，现有调度器虽然平均每过境多获得1-2幅图像，但在1/4到1/3的单独过境中反而少于均匀功率方案；即使拥有精确天气信息的"精灵"（genie）也无法改善这一比率。这验证了平均指标掩盖了严重的个别失败风险。

Conclusion: 调度评价应从"平均增益"转向"单次失败率"；安全裕度应基于历史反噬频率来校准，而非统计可靠性。该框架能对不同编码器的可调度性做出认证判断，宁可拒绝学习型编码器，也要保证调度方案在个体过境层面几乎不劣于基准方案。

Abstract: A low-Earth-orbit satellite has only minutes to downlink imagery on a limited power budget, and some moments in a pass carry far better channels than others. Spending more power on the good moments and skipping hopeless ones should deliver more images, but the channel cannot be measured in time, so the schedule is planned in advance from weather statistics using a safety margin. That margin is sized so the forecast is statistically reliable, and the scheduler is judged by whether it beats uniform power on average. Both targets are wrong for a satellite that gets one attempt per pass: on real Sentinel-2 imagery, a scheduler gaining one to two images per pass on average still delivers fewer than uniform power on a quarter to a third of individual passes, and a genie knowing the weather exactly does no better. We instead size the margin by measuring, on recorded passes, how often it would have backfired, keeping only settings that provably backfire rarely. The procedure declines to schedule our learned codec and certifies scheduling for codecs with a sharper cliff.

</details>


### [34] [SheafIQ: Sheaf-Theoretic Information Quantification of Vector Fields on Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08728)
*Cong Shen,Guancen Lin,Chuan-Shen Hu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出SheafIQ，一个基于层论（sheaf theory）的框架，用于量化图结构上向量值状态的空间组织。它通过将相邻向量映射到公共边坐标系、将局部不相容性转化为残差能量分布，并用熵来度量其全局组织性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要分别刻画图拓扑或个体信号，无法量化节点间向量交互在整张图上的组织方式。这限制了我们对生物和工程系统中向量值信号网络的理解。

Method: SheafIQ框架包含三个步骤：(1) 将相邻节点上的向量映射到边关联的公共坐标系；(2) 将局部不相容性转化为残差能量分布；(3) 通过熵来量化该能量分布的全局组织特征。

Result: 在蛋白质、功能性脑网络、城市交通系统和电网等多个系统中，SheafIQ一致地揭示了超越传统图与信号描述符的互补性组织信息。

Conclusion: SheafIQ建立了一个统一的信息论框架，用于量化几何图上向量值状态的组织性，将网络分析从仅关注图拓扑扩展到包含节点向量状态的空间组织。

Abstract: Vector fields on graph structures naturally arise in diverse biological and engineered systems, where vector-valued states are defined on the nodes and evolve through the network interactions. Existing methods primarily characterize either the graph topology or individual signals, but generally do not quantify how local interactions among node-associated vectors are organized across the graph. To address this limitation, a sheaf-theoretic framework, termed SheafIQ, is proposed to represent neighboring vectors in a common edge-associated coordinate system, map local incompatibilities to a residual energy distribution, and quantify its global organization through entropy. Across proteins, functional brain networks, urban traffic systems, and power grids, SheafIQ consistently reveals complementary organizational information beyond conventional graph- and signal-based descriptors. More broadly, it establishes a unified information-theoretic framework for quantifying the organization of vector-valued states on geometric graphs, extending network analysis beyond graph topology alone.

</details>

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication is turning the cellular infrastructure into an active observer of the physical world. When such infrastructure interacts with embodied agents capable of deliberately changing their states and surroundings, the physical world itself can become a communication medium. This paper studies the fundamental communication limits of this sensing-induced embodied communication paradigm in the near field. We consider an agent that maps messages to the positions of a controllable scatterer within a bounded three-dimensional (3D) region, while a base station decodes the selected position from multi-snapshot monostatic sensing echoes. Near-field spherical wavefronts resolve both angle and range, expanding the embodied-symbol space from a 2D plane to a 3D volume. This gain, however, comes with a position-dependent and anisotropic reliability geometry. We characterize this geometry through the pairwise Bhattacharyya distance and derive a local ellipsoidal representation of the resulting 3D confusability regions, whose principal axes quantify directional sensing resolution. The ellipsoid further degenerates into the 2D transverse ellipse in the far-field limit, unifying the two regimes. We then formulate the finite-snapshot $ε$-capacity and translate reliable codebook design into a 3D packing problem. A face-centered cubic construction provides an achievable rate, while a geometric converse yields a complementary upper bound. Numerical results validate the proposed geometry and demonstrate the capacity gain of near-field volumetric packing over far-field planar packing. These results establish a unified geometric and information-theoretic framework for communication through deliberately configured physical states.

</details>

Result: 建立了CPECC码的两类新上界，构造了多个新最优CPECC码族，完全解决了先前文献中关于CPECC码的猜想，并获得了LPECC码的新上界与新最优族，揭示了LPECC与CPECC最优码之间的维度对应关系。

Abstract: The low-power error-correcting cooling (LPECC) codes and constant-power error-correcting cooling (CPECC) codes, introduced in [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, 64 (2018), 3062--3085; 66 (2020), 4804--4818], respectively, are two coding schemes designed to simultaneously control the peak temperature and average power consumption of on-chip buses while providing error-correction capability for transmitted information. This paper establishes new upper bounds for both $(n,1,w,w-2)$-CPECC codes and $(n,t,w,w-2)$-CPECC codes using graph-theoretic techniques, and constructs several new families of optimal CPECC codes using combinatorial configurations. Moreover, it completely resolves the conjecture concerning CPECC codes posed in [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, DOI: 10.1109/TIT.2026.3721101]. Finally, we derive a new upper bound for $(n,t,w,w-2)$-LPECC codes by probabilistic method, along with new optimal families, and establish the relationship between optimal $(n,t,w,w-2)$-LPECC codes and optimal $(n+1,t,w,w-2)$-CPECC codes.

Abstract: Universally optimal (UO) codes were introduced by H. Cohn and A. Kumar in 2007 and later extended to the discrete setting by Cohn and Y. Zhao. They minimize the ``energy'' among all codes of the same size for a certain class of potential functions. So far only a small number of specific functionals have been linked to information theory problems. We add one more, showing that UO codes optimize $α$-mutual information for $α=2$ in the context of wiretap channels with a noiseless main channel. This implies that UO codes are optimal for transmission over this type of wiretap channels.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 和秩度量码在网络编码、空时码和分布式存储中有重要应用，MSRD码是达到和秩度量Singleton界的良好码，但某些维度下MSRD码不存在。因此需要引入更一般化的概念来弥补这一缺失。

Method: 引入QMSRD码的概念并研究其性质；发现并非所有QMSRD码的对偶码也是QMSRD，因此定义对偶QMSRD码；通过刻画这类码的结构，推导其支撑、秩列表与和秩分布，并计算广义和秩权重。

Conclusion: QMSRD码及对偶QMSRD码为MSRD码不存在的情况提供了替代的良好码类，丰富了和秩度量码的理论体系，具有潜在的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Sum-rank-metric codes have recently attracted considerable attention of many researchers, due to their applications in network coding, space-time codes and distributed storage. MSRD codes are those good codes attaining the Singleton bound in the sum-rank metric. However, MSRD codes do not exist for some dimensions. Motivated by this fact, we introduce the notion of quasi-MSRD (QMSRD) codes and provide some properties of them. What is more, we find that not every QMSRD code has a QMSRD dual code, so we give the definition of dually QMSRD codes, whose dual codes and themselves are both QMSRD. Finally, we characterize such codes, derive their support, rank-list and sum-rank distributions as well as compute their generalized sum-rank weights.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 建立联合系统模型，整合FU算法的收敛行为、本地设备计算动态以及有界CSI误差下的最坏情况鲁棒传输模型；将非凸联合资源分配问题利用约束的单调性和凸性进行分解，通过局部精度参数的均匀扫描，在每个子问题内使用嵌套二分搜索和黄金分割搜索确定最优延迟、带宽、功率和计算频率。

Result: 理论分析和大量数值结果表明，所提算法实现了多项式复杂度，与常规基线方案相比显著减少了整体遗忘完成时间。

Conclusion: 该算法在保证多项式复杂度的同时，能有效优化无线联邦遗忘网络的资源分配，大幅降低遗忘任务完成延迟。

Abstract: To comply with stringent data privacy regulations, federated unlearning (FU) has emerged as a critical paradigm. However, its implementation over wireless networks introduces severe communication latency and reliability challenges due to iterative calibration requirements and physical-layer channel uncertainties. In this paper, we investigate the problem of delay minimization for federated unlearning networks (FUN). Specifically, we establish a comprehensive system model that jointly incorporates the convergence behavior of the FUN algorithm, local device computation dynamics, and a worst-case robust transmission model operating under bounded channel state information (CSI) error. To solve the resulting non-convex joint resource allocation problem, we propose an efficient iterative algorithm. By exploiting the monotonicity and convexity properties of the system constraints, the problem is decomposed via a uniform scan over the local accuracy parameter, within which the optimal delay, bandwidth, power, and computation frequency are determined utilizing nested bisection and golden-section searches. Both theoretical analysis and extensive numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieves polynomial complexity and significantly reduces the overall unlearning completion time compared to conventional baseline schemes.

</details>


### [40] [Quantum Bicycle LDPC Codes with High $kd^2/n$ from Divisor-Driven Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09115)
*Liangdong Lu,Guanmin Guo,Yang Liu,Ruipan Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明循环双块循环（bicycle）量子LDPC码可简化为多项式环F2[x]/(x^l-1)上的构造：自正交性自动成立，量子维度可由多项式gcd直接读出，最小距离通过Calderbank对应精确验证。基于此框架的搜索恢复了[[42,12,4]]和[[62,12,4]]等短码，并产生多个具有竞争力的码，如[[66,20,7]]（kd²/n=14.85，优于双变量bicycle码[[144,12,12]]），同时在n=48处通过穷举刻画了该构造的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双块循环量子LDPC码包含一些已知最优的小型量子码，但其设计一直依赖群代数方法，维度与距离需通过矩阵计算才能获得，缺乏代数上的简洁刻画，限制了系统化搜索。作者希望将构造转化为更简单的代数框架，实现高效的码搜索。

Method: 将循环情况下的双块循环码构造归约到多项式环F2[x]/(x^l-1)：(1)证明自正交性自动满足；(2)量子维度由多项式gcd直接读出；(3)通过Calderbank对应精确验证最小距离，将码搜索转化为代数预过滤的枚举方法。基于该框架进行计算机搜索，并在n=48进行穷举普查。

Result: 恢复了短码[[42,12,4]]和[[62,12,4]]；产生竞争性码族，包括[[66,20,7]]（kd²/n=14.85，块长不及[[144,12,12]]一半但品质因数更高）、[[46,2,8]]、[[66,2,9]]、[[66,4,8]]、[[66,6,8]]，以及n=90的[[90,16,6]]、[[90,18,6]]、[[90,20,6]]。n=48穷举发现[[48,10,6]]码（仅需48元素的最小群，而Aydin-Tamo-Barg实现需72元素），并证明距离5会导致稳定子秩损失，排除了[[48,10,5]]。

Conclusion: 该框架为bicycle型量子LDPC码开辟了超越群论搜索范畴的系统化构造路径，并精确定位了真正需要coset理论的现象边界，为量子码设计提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: Bicycle (two-block circulant) quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes include some of the best known small quantum codes, yet their design has relied on group-algebra formulations in which the dimension and distance are accessible only through matrix computation. We show that in the cyclic case the construction collapses into the polynomial ring $\F_2[x]/(x^{l}-1)$: self-orthogonality is automatic, the quantum dimension is read off from a polynomial gcd, and the minimum distance is certified exactly through the Calderbank correspondence to additive codes over $\F_4$, turning code search into an algebraically pre-filtered enumeration that reaches parameter regimes poorly covered by existing tables. A computer search based on this framework recovers the short codes $[[42,12,4]]_2$ and $[[62,12,4]]_2$ and produces a family of codes with competitive figure of merit $kd^2/n$, including $[[66,20,7]]_2$ with $kd^2/n=14.85$, above the bivariate bicycle code $[[144,12,12]]_2$ ($kd^2/n=12$) at less than half the block length, together with $[[46,2,8]]_2$, $[[66,2,9]]_2$, $[[66,4,8]]_2$, $[[66,6,8]]_2$ and, at $n=90$, $[[90,16,6]]_2$, $[[90,18,6]]_2$, $[[90,20,6]]_2$. An exhaustive census at $n=48$ delineates the boundary of this picture: we exhibit a $[[48,10,6]]_2$ code from a minimal $48$-element group (the Aydin--Tamo--Barg realization uses $72$ elements), and prove that distance $5$ forces a stabilizer-rank loss, which excludes $[[48,10,5]]_2$ from the weight-$8$ symmetric coset family. The framework thus opens a systematic route to bicycle-type quantum LDPC codes beyond the reach of group-theoretic searches, and identifies exactly where genuinely coset-theoretic phenomena begin.

</details>

*Wooseok Cha,Kyeongsoo Kim,Seonghoon Kim,Junil Choi,Jihwan P. Choi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低地球轨道（LEO）卫星网络的协作传输框架，联合考虑非均匀业务需求和网络功率消耗，通过混合预编码、射频链激活和硬件量化，结合用户中心卫星聚类，最大化需求感知的能量效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络具有广阔覆盖但业务需求空间非均匀，同时星上功率约束严格，需要能效优先的传输策略来有效利用稀缺资源。

Method: 提出协作传输框架：每颗LEO卫星集成混合预编码（HPC）、射频链激活和硬件量化；基于统计信道状态信息（sCSI）和业务需求组织UE中心卫星簇；联合优化传输架构与资源分配以最大化需求感知EE，形成MINLP问题；开发两阶段算法，采用改进的交叉熵（CE）方法搜索离散变量，用分数规划进行发射功率分配。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提框架在业务需求和能效方面均优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的协作传输框架能有效兼顾非均匀流量需求与网络能效，为LEO卫星网络资源优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks are envisioned as a promising solution for providing ubiquitous connectivity and narrowing the digital divide. The extensive footprint of LEO satellite constellations enables broad coverage, resulting in spatially non-uniform traffic demand across the serviced areas. Meanwhile, stringent on-board power constraints make power-intensive transmission architectures less attractive and motivate energy-efficient transmission strategies that effectively exploit scarce satellite network resources. To this end, this paper proposes a cooperative transmission framework that jointly accounts for non-uniform traffic demand and network-wide power consumption. Each LEO satellite integrates hybrid precoding (HPC), radio frequency (RF) chain activation, and hardware quantization, while user-equipment (UE)-centric satellite clusters are organized using statistical channel state information (sCSI) and traffic demands. A framework for joint optimization of cooperative transmission architecture and resource allocation is designed to maximize demand-aware energy efficiency (EE), resulting in a mixed-integer nonlinear program (MINLP) for which finding a globally optimal solution is generally intractable. Accordingly, a two-stage algorithm is developed under a distributed linear precoding structure, in which a modified cross-entropy (CE) method searches over discrete variables, while fractional programming is employed for transmit power allocation. Numerical results indicate that the proposed framework outperforms benchmark schemes while accounting for traffic demands and EE.

</details>


### [42] [Optimal Movable-Antenna Control for Multi-Path Sensing Guided by Prior AoA Statistic (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09165)
*Jaehong Kim,Changsheng You,Jihong Park,Seung-Woo Ko*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于先验信息的敏捷多径感知框架，利用弱先验到达角（AoA）统计信息，通过一次可移动板方向优化和两次线性扫描，在显著降低控制开销与感知延迟的同时，实现了接近单径基准的多径AoA与到达时间（ToA）估计精度，适用于6G可移动天线（MA）感知场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MA多径感知方法通常需要对整个可移动区域进行穷举扫描，导致控制开销大、感知延迟高，难以满足6G敏捷感知的实际需求。

Method: 该框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 基于Fisher信息分析，仅优化一次可移动板的三维方向，配置机械上可行的扫描区域，以增强路径可见性并保持路径间可区分性；(2) 在最优板方向下，MA仅执行两次线性扫描，将其非共线空间相位投影与先验AoA统计通过最大后验（MAP）估计器融合，恢复多径的仰角和方位角AoA，随后利用估计的AoA增强目标路径分量并抑制其他路径干扰，提取ToA。

Result: 仅需一次方向调整和两次线性扫描即可实现敏捷的多径感知，显著降低控制开销与延迟，且AoA和ToA估计精度接近单径基准。

Conclusion: 该先验引导的敏捷多径感知框架有效解决了MA感知中开销大、延迟高的问题，为6G多径感知提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-path sensing, which aims to extract the geometric attributes of multiple propagation paths, is expected to be a key functionality of 6G. A movable antenna (MA) can enable this functionality by synthesizing an aperture through mechanical motion. However, existing MA-based sensing methods typically rely on exhaustive scanning over the entire movable region, resulting in significant control overhead and sensing latency, which limit their practicality for agile sensing. To address this challenge, this paper develops a prior-guided agile multi-path sensing framework that leverages weak prior angle-of-arrival (AoA) statistics as side information. The proposed framework is built on two key steps. First, the movable plate's three-dimensional orientation is optimized only once to configure a mechanically feasible scan region that enhances path visibility while preserving inter-path discriminability, guided by Fisher information analysis. By decomposing line-of-sight (LoS) and non-line-of-sight (NLoS) components and integrating UAV motion dynamics with FAS port activation states, the proposed model accurately characterizes the non-uniform spatial distribution of near-field channels. The subarray partition scheme dynamically groups active ports to satisfy near-field conditions while significantly reducing computational complexity, supported by a dynamic update algorithm that efficiently handles subarray adjustments during port switching. To avoid low effective gain and deep-fading ports in dense FAS configurations, a channel gain-based selection strategy is employed to prioritize high-gain ports. We derive and analyze the modeling accuracy and channel capacity, investigating the impact of FAS dimensions, port spacing, active port count, and UAV dynamics on system performance. Abstract: We derive the four principal asymptotic rate-distance tradeoffs for binary codes---Plotkin, Elias--Bassalygo, and the two McEliece--Rodemich--Rumsey--Welch (MRRW) bounds---from one theorem, the ``pretty good criterion.'' If the bit error rate under the pretty good measurement (PGM)---the quantum analog of posterior sampling---of a binary-input output-symmetric classical--quantum (cq) channel lies below $δ$, then every length-$n$ binary code, linear or nonlinear, of relative distance $δ$ has rate at most the channel's capacity, up to an $O(n^{-1/2})$ correction. Rate--distance bounds thereby reduce to a channel design problem, wherein the task is to minimize channel capacity subject to the posterior bit error rate constraint. Via the pretty good criterion, the binary erasure channel (BEC) yields Plotkin, the binary symmetric channel (BSC) yields Elias--Bassalygo, the pure-state channel (PSC) yields the first MRRW bound, and a masked PSC yields the second MRRW bound exactly.
  This framework is then instantiated with new channels to improve upon the MRRW bounds. Specifically, the mixed-qubit channel (MQC), a mixed-state version of PSC, strictly improves the first MRRW bound at every $0 < δ< \frac{1}{2}$, while the masked mixed-qubit channel (2MQC) strictly improves the second MRRW bound throughout the same interval.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出轨道检测（OD）框架，通过将离散符号先验松弛为混合离散-连续密度，设计渐近最优、低复杂度的MIMO消息传递接收机。该框架将符号分布分解为离散径向分量（仅支持L个幅度环）和连续最大熵相位密度，将后验均值和方差无损压缩为3L个实标量，并将最优O(M)复杂度的去噪器化简为O(L)乃至O(1)的闭合层级（OBD、OGD、OPD）。利用Jacobi-Anger阶梯可恢复精确检测器，并给出了五个信息论结果，包括相同的SE不动点、误差衰减律从指数到线性的变化、速率损失指数衰减、MMSE维度d=1/2，以及Wasserstein距离下的最优传输界。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO系统中，最优消息传递接收机的复杂度随星座大小M呈线性增长，在高阶调制下计算负担过重。现有低复杂度方案往往牺牲最优性或引入误差平层。作者希望设计一种既渐近最优又具有极低复杂度的接收机框架，并系统分析其信息论性能边界。

Method: 将离散符号先验松弛为混合离散-连续密度（轨道先验），将每个符号分布分解为L个幅度环上的离散径向分量和连续最大熵相位密度。由此提出三级去噪器层级：OBD（Bessel去噪器）、OGD（无Bessel的高斯去噪器）和OPD（相位去噪器），复杂度从O(L)降至O(1)。利用Jacobi-Anger展开构造恢复精确检测器的阶梯。通过状态演化（SE）分析、误差衰减律分析、MMSE维度分析以及Wasserstein最优传输界进行理论刻画。

Result: OBD、OGD和OPD共享相同的首阶SE不动点；高SNR下误差衰减律从指数变为线性但不会形成误差平层；任何欠载系统中速率损失随SNR指数衰减，每个电平均渐近达到log2 M容量；OD达到MMSE维度d=1/2（介于BOD的d=0和线性接收机的d=1之间）；建立了星座环几何与可达速率之间的Wasserstein最优传输界。

Conclusion: 轨道检测框架实现了渐近最优性与极低复杂度（O(1)）的统一，为大规模MIMO高阶调制场景提供了实用的接收机设计。其信息论分析表明，以高SNR下的误差衰减律从指数变为线性为代价，换取了容量渐近性、无误差平层以及中间态MMSE维度等优良性质。该框架为MIMO接收机设计提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: We introduce orbital detection (OD), a framework for designing asymptotically optimal, low-complexity message passing (MP) receivers for digitally modulated multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems, based on relaxing the discrete symbol prior into a mixed discrete-continuous density. The resulting orbital prior factors each symbol's distribution into a discrete radial component, supported on only the \(L << M\) amplitude rings of an arbitrary constellation \(\mathcal{M}\) of cardinality \(M = |\mathcal{M}|\), and a continuous, maximum-entropy phase density on each ring. This compresses the propagated posterior mean and variance losslessly into \(3L\) real scalars, and collapses the optimal \(\mathcal{O}(M)\)-complexity denoiser into a closed-form hierarchy whose per-symbol cost falls to \(\mathcal{O}(L)\) and ultimately \(\mathcal{O}(1)\): the orbital Bessel denoiser (OBD), its Bessel-free variant the orbital Gaussian denoiser (OGD), and the orbital phase denoiser (OPD), proved irreducible on the ring manifold. A Jacobi-Anger ladder recovers the exact detector with geometrically vanishing error. Five information-theoretic results follow. First, the OBD, OGD, and OPD share an identical leading-order state evolution (SE) fixed point. Second, the sole price is a change in the high-SNR error-decay law, from exponential to linear, which never hardens into an error floor. Third, for any underloaded system the induced rate loss vanishes exponentially in SNR, so every level is asymptotically capacity-achieving in the constellation-constrained sense, attaining \(\log_2 M\). Fourth, OD attains a minimum mean square error (MMSE) dimension \(d=1/2\), halfway between the \(d=0\) Bayes-optimal denoiser (BOD) and the \(d=1\) linear receiver. Fifth, a non-asymptotic optimal-transport bound in Wasserstein distance links constellation ring geometry directly to the achievable rate.

</details>

Result: 在单链路实验测试台上，恢复的去极化概率准确，估计的过程保真度在多种光纤长度和波长下紧密跟踪贝叶斯过程层析基线；星型网络估计器在基于实测单链路信道的仿真路径上得到验证；树型和环形网格网络的蒙特卡洛模拟验证了推广算法的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum-classical coexistence networks (QCNs) share optical fiber between quantum and classical signals via wavelength-division multiplexing, offering a practical path to quantum communication over existing telecom infrastructure. However, co- and counter-propagating classical traffic introduce distinct depolarization noise, complicating channel characterization. We develop a tomography framework that infers per-link channel parameters of a QCN from end-to-end measurements alone. We first model each coexisting fiber by decomposing the signal evolution into photon loss, successful transmission, and three direction-dependent depolarization components. We then derive closed-form link-level estimators, and extend the approach to star-topology networks through a system of multiplicative equations across end-node pairs, together with a simple classical-signal-direction-switching protocol that resolves the remaining unknowns. On single-link experimental testbed data, we recover per-link depolarization probabilities accurately, with estimated process fidelities closely tracking the Bayesian-process-tomography baseline across multiple fiber lengths and wavelengths; residual gaps reflect the depolarization-only approximation. Absent a multi-link coexistence testbed, we validate the star-network estimators on emulated paths built from measured single-link channels. We further extend the framework in two directions: (i) a channel model that factorizes the coexisting fiber into a depolarizing-with-loss signal channel and a Raman-noise-injection channel on separate optical modes -- a completely-positive, trace-preserving tensor product -- whose link observables reduce exactly to our basic model; and (ii) a generalization to arbitrary topologies via a peeling algorithm (trees) and a least-squares estimator (meshes), validated by Monte-Carlo simulations on tree and cyclic-mesh networks.

Abstract: Recent advancements in wireless endogenous security have explored leveraging the inherent randomness of wireless channels to enhance communication security, providing an effective alternative to traditional encryption methods. This paper proposes a wiretap coding scheme within the semantic communication framework, which leverages discrete semantic representations compatible with conventional digital modulation to jointly enhance communication security and reliability. We investigate two eavesdropping scenarios: (i) the eavesdropper employs a maximum a posteriori (MAP) decoder, and (ii) the eavesdropper has access to a decoder identical to that of the legitimate receiver. In the first scenario, we exploit mutual information as a metric to guide the design of an optimized coding strategy, minimizing information leakage while enhancing communication reliability. In the second scenario, considering the limitations of the eavesdropper's decoding capability, we employ generalized mutual information (GMI) to characterize recoverability under the prescribed decoding rule and guide reliability-aware code optimization.

Abstract: Multi-camera wireless perception requires a base station (BS) to maintain timely and reliable latent beliefs from distributed cameras under limited uplink resources. Conventional Age-of-Information (AoI) measures the age of the latest received update but not task-relevant latent content. We introduce Age-of-Latent (AoL) to quantify the freshness of each camera's latest decoded latent representation. A finite temporal window of integration (TWI) determines the task-commitment time and available uplink slots, creating a tradeoff among update opportunities, prediction duration, commit-time AoL, prediction reliability, and accumulated resource cost. Within this horizon, redundant or overlapping views enable correlated prediction, reducing reliance on the highest-cost communication and encoding configuration to maintain BS-side latent beliefs. We formulate a joint AoL-resource minimization problem coupling task-level TWI selection with slot-level encoder selection, scheduling, and NOMA power allocation under prediction-reliability constraints. We propose correlation-aware latent prediction for AoL minimization (CoLA), which uses Lyapunov optimization for task-level TWI selection based on AoL-resource cost and prediction uncertainty, and proximal policy optimization for slot-level resource control. Results on a warehouse multi-camera RF dataset show that CoLA adapts the TWI to camera-update availability and achieves the most favorable AoL-resource tradeoff among the benchmarks while maintaining prediction reliability, particularly under prolonged and severe camera outages.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAS利用大量候选端口实现空间分集，但硬件限制仅允许少数活跃端口进行信道观测。现有方法通常需要预先获取信道统计信息或依赖完整端口CSI，这在无先验统计知识的情况下是否可行尚未明确，因此需要探索无需预先统计信息的全端口CSI恢复方法。

Method: 在Clarke各向同性散射模型下，证明信道位于由散射环境决定的低维空间模态子空间；基于此建立当观测端口数M≥r（模态维度）时CSI可恢复的理论条件，并推导出恢复不可行的尖锐阈值；通过将恢复误差分解为模态截断、估计和学习三个组成部分，分析各系统参数间的显式权衡关系。

Result: 建立了可靠的可行性阈值：当M低于模态维度时，无论信噪比多高都无法可靠恢复；当M超过该维度时，增加观测可提升精度。推导出RF链数量、导频开销、发射功率和训练数据之间明确的权衡关系，实现少数活跃端口下无需先验统计的全端口CSI可扩展恢复。

Conclusion: 该研究从理论上揭示了FAS中信道恢复的本质维度取决于散射环境而非端口总数，为无需统计先验信息的可扩展全端口CSI恢复提供了理论基础，对实际FAS系统设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FASs) exploit many candidate ports for spatial diversity, but hardware constraints allow channel observations at only a few active ports. Whether full-port CSI can be recovered without pre-acquired channel statistics remains open. Under the Clarke isotropic scattering model, we show that the channel lies in a low-dimensional spatial modal subspace determined by the scattering environment rather than the total port count. Consequently, recovery becomes feasible when the number of observed ports reaches the modal dimension (i.e., $M\geq r$), even when $M\ll N$. We further establish a sharp feasibility threshold: reliable recovery is impossible below this dimension regardless of SNR, whereas accuracy improves with additional observations above it. By decomposing the recovery error into modal truncation, estimation, and learning components, we derive explicit tradeoffs among RF chains, pilot overhead, transmit power, and training data. These results enable scalable prior-free full-port CSI recovery with few active ports.

</details>


### [50] [Separating Abelian and Homomorphic Entropy Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09543)
*Shahram Khazaei*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种受限群特征化熵锥：阿贝尔锥 Γ̃^Abl_n 和同态锥 Γ̃^Hom_n（由正规子群的陪集系统生成）。作者证明了 Γ̃^Abl_16 ⊊ Γ̃^Hom_16，并将两锥不同的最小变量数 n_AH 限定在 6 到 16 之间。分离泛函是一种类受限熵不等式，通过提升 Pálfy–Szabó 六交叉恒等式的序对偶并量化不精确子群交的误差获得。作者构造了 2^43 阶二类 2-群的 16 个正规子群，使所有交误差消失而端点包含关系相差一位。由于混合线性随机变量属于阿贝尔类，该例子也分离了混合线性熵锥与同态熵锥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Chan 和 Yeung 已证明有限群足以确定哪些齐次线性信息不等式是普适有效的。本文旨在进一步理解不同受限群结构（阿贝尔群与同态/陪集系统）所生成的熵锥之间的差异，从而更精细地刻画信息不等式在不同代数约束下的有效范围。

Method: 通过提升 Pálfy–Szabó 六交叉恒等式的序对偶来构造分离泛函，并显式量化不精确子群交处的误差项。作者构造了一个 2^43 阶二类 2-群及其 16 个正规子群的具体例子，验证所有交误差为零，但端点包含关系仍失败一位，从而证明两锥不等。

Result: 证明 Γ̃^Abl_16 ⊊ Γ̃^Hom_16，并给出 6 ≤ n_AH ≤ 16 的界。分离不等式是类受限的：在阿贝尔锥上有效但非普适信息不等式。构造的群论例子同时分离了混合线性熵锥与同态熵锥。

Conclusion: 阿贝尔熵锥严格包含于同态熵锥（在至少 16 个变量时成立），说明同态结构允许更丰富的信息关系。这为理解不同代数结构约束下的熵锥层级提供了新的洞见，并揭示了混合线性随机变量与更一般同态结构之间的本质差异。

Abstract: Chan and Yeung showed that finite groups suffice to determine which homogeneous linear information inequalities are universally valid. We compare two restricted group-characterizable entropy cones: the Abelian cone $\widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Abl}}_n$ and the homomorphic cone $\widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Hom}}_n$, the latter generated by coset systems of normal subgroups. We prove \[
  \widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Abl}}_{16}\subsetneq\widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Hom}}_{16}, \] and, if $n_{\rm AH}$ is the least number of variables for which these cones differ, we show $6\le n_{\rm AH}\le16$. The separating functional is a class-restricted entropy inequality: it is valid on the Abelian cone but is not a universal information inequality. It is obtained by lifting the order dual of the Pálfy--Szabó six-cross identity while quantifying errors at inexact subgroup joins. We then construct sixteen normal subgroups of a class-two $2$-group of order $2^{43}$ for which every join error vanishes while the endpoint containment fails by one bit. Since mixed-linear random variables are Abelian, the same example also separates the mixed-linear and homomorphic entropy cones.

</details>


### [51] [Row-Boosted Ensemble Belief Propagation for Short LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09560)
*Paul Bezner,Felix Krieg,Stephan ten Brink*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出行增强集成（RBE）解码方法，通过在所有集成成员共享相同校验矩阵和BP内核的基础上，仅对少量校验行的传出消息进行增强，来克服短/中长LDPC码有限长度图效应导致的BP解码性能限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短和中长LDPC码的BP解码受有限长度图效应限制，单一解码轨迹可能陷入陷阱或振荡，即使存在替代轨迹可以成功解码。现有的集成BP解码器需要多种多样化的机制（多种校验矩阵、自同构、修改调度等）来创造解码多样性，但这些机制较为复杂。

Method: 提出行增强集成（RBE）解码：所有集成成员共享相同的校验矩阵和BP内核，仅对一小部分校验行中传出的消息进行增强（boosted），以此作为最小的解码侧多样性机制。

Result: 在5G NR BG1 (144,96)码上，32个成员的RBE将BP-20的误帧率从1.6×10⁻²降至1.6×10⁻³（E_b/N_0=4.0dB），优于同规模的饱和最小和与仿射子码集成。增加集成规模可进一步获得增益，性能提升可跨5G NR块长度和速率、非5G短LDPC码以及洪泛和分层调度转移。

Conclusion: RBE提供了一种可扩展的性能-复杂度权衡机制，是一种简单有效的解码侧多样性方法，能够显著提升短和中长LDPC码的BP解码性能。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding of short and moderate-length low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes is limited by finite-length graph effects: a single decoder trajectory can become trapped or oscillatory even when an alternative trajectory would decode the received word. Existing ensemble-BP decoders create the required diversity through multiple parity-check matrices, automorphisms, modified schedules, subcodes, or altered update rules. We introduce row-boosted ensemble (RBE) decoding as a minimal decoder-side diversity mechanism: all ensemble members share the same parity-check matrix and the same BP kernel, and differ only in a small set of parity-check rows whose outgoing messages are boosted. On the 5G~NR BG1 \((144,96)\) code, RBE with \(32\) members lowers the frame-error rate of BP with \(20\) iterations (BP-20) from \(1.6\times10^{-2}\) to \(1.6\times10^{-3}\) at \(E_\mathrm{b}/N_0=4.0\,\mathrm{dB}\), outperforming saturated-min-sum and affine subcode ensembles of equal size. Increasing the ensemble size yields additional gains, indicating that RBE provides a scalable performance-complexity tradeoff. The gains transfer across 5G~NR block lengths and rates, to non-5G short LDPC codes, and across flooding and layered schedules.

</details>


### [52] [SECS: A Soft Ensemble-Combining Stage for Low-Latency Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09563)
*Felix Krieg,Paul Bezner,Stephan ten Brink*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种软集成合并阶段（SECS），利用浅层多样化置信传播（BP）解码器仅运行少量迭代即可产生近乎无错的高可靠性排序，配合后续重编码阶段（如OSD）实现接近最大似然（ML）解码的性能，同时大幅减少所需的BP迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集成解码依赖从多个BP解码候选中选择输出，但一旦所有解码器均未找到正确码字则解码失败。作者观察到浅层多样化的BP成员虽不是好的纠错器，却是优秀的排序器，因此希望利用这一特性改进集成解码方案。

Method: 提出SECS（软集成合并阶段）：让多样化的BP解码器仅运行少量迭代后合并外信息，产生可靠性排序（最可靠位置近乎无错）；随后附加重编码阶段（如有序统计解码OSD）将该排序转换为接近ML的候选码字。

Result: 在三种短码上验证：（63,30）BCH码、过完备PG(2,8)码、以及搜索设计的（105,53）循环码。所有情况下，SECS配合OSD后处理都弥补了与ML解码之间的大部分差距，同时显著减少了所需BP迭代次数。

Conclusion: 浅层多样化BP成员作为"排序器"而非"纠错器"的异构使用方式，能够以更低的延迟和更少的迭代次数获得接近ML解码的性能，为超可靠低时延通信中的集成解码提供了一种高效新方案。

Abstract: Ensemble decoding is a promising technique for ultra-reliable low-latency communication, as it trades hardware parallelism for decoding latency by running M diverse belief propagation (BP) decoders in parallel. Conventional ensembles select their output from the candidates. Hence, decoding fails whenever no member finds the correct codeword. In this work, we show that shallow, diverse BP members are poor correctors, but excellent sorters. Based on this observation, we propose a soft ensemble-combining stage (SECS) that combines extrinsic messages after only a few iterations, yielding a reliability ordering whose most reliable positions are nearly error-free. A subsequent re-encoding stage (like ordered-statistics decoding (OSD)) converts this ordering into a near-maximum likelihood (ML) candidate codeword, at a fraction of the BP latency of a fully converged ensemble. We demonstrate the proposed scheme on three short codes: a (63,30) Bose-Ray-Chaudhuri-Hocquenghem code, an overcomplete PG(2,8) code, and a search-designed (105,53) cyclic code. In all cases, the SECS with OSD post-processing closes most of the gap to ML decoding, while significantly reducing the number of required BP iterations.

</details>

*Marvin Rübenacke,Ryan Seah,Warren J. Gross*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对极化码的高吞吐、低延迟译码需求，提出了基于汉明距离和椭球距离的新接受准则来改进推测式连续消除（Spec-SC）译码的接受率，并进一步将码成员测试与推测式译码结合以加速高码率节点。数值结果表明，所提方法在延迟上大幅优于快速简化SC（FSSC）译码和原始Spec-SC，且错误率性能几乎相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代数据通道应用对极化码译码算法提出了高吞吐和低延迟的要求。已有的Spec-SC译码通过并行推测执行右分支来降低平均译码延迟，但其接受率有限，尤其是对低码率节点而言，速度提升受限。因此需要改进接受准则以最大化加速效果。

Method: (1) 提出基于汉明距离和椭球距离的新接受准则，提高低码率节点的推测接受率；(2) 将码成员测试（code membership testing）与推测式译码相结合，以加速高码率节点的推测执行。

Result: 数值结果证实，所提出的两种方法在延迟方面均大幅优于FSSC译码和采用原始接受条件的Spec-SC译码，同时错误率性能几乎与基线一致。

Conclusion: 通过改进接受准则（汉明距离/椭球距离）和引入码成员测试，分别提升了低码率和高码率节点的推测译码加速效果，实现了延迟的大幅降低而不损失错误率性能，适用于高性能极化码数据通道应用。

Abstract: Next-generation data-channel applications of polar codes demand decoding algorithms with high throughput and low latency. The recently proposed speculative successive cancellation (Spec-SC) decoding reduces average decoding latency by speculatively executing the right branch of successive cancellation (SC) decoding in parallel with the left branch, verifying the result once the g-function is computed. In this paper, we propose new acceptance criteria based on Hamming distance and ellipsoidal distance that improve acceptance rates, enabling greater speed-ups for lower-rate nodes. We further show that combining code membership testing with speculative decoding accelerates high-rate nodes as well. Numerical results confirm that both approaches outperform fast simplified SC (FSSC) decoding and Spec-SC with the original acceptance condition in terms latency by a wide margin, with virtually identical error-rate performance.

</details>


### [54] [Entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes: bounds and constructions with availability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09886)
*Gretchen L. Matthews,Julia Shapiro*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文定义了具有可用性的纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码（EA-qLRC with availability），其中任意至多δ-1个删除的量子比特可以从t个局部恢复集中的任意一个恢复，每个局部恢复集大小至多r+δ-1。共享纠缠使得t>1成为可能，即同一组删除可以有多个可用的局部恢复集。作者建立了该码族的Singleton型界限，并基于范德蒙德校验矩阵的经典线性码给出了随机构造，还从多个经典码族（Tamo-Barg码、纤维积码、代数几何码如Hermitian和Suzuki单点码）及其折叠版本提供了显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子局部可恢复码在量子存储和分布式量子计算中具有重要意义，但现有工作缺乏对具有多个可用恢复集（availability）的纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码的系统研究。共享纠缠作为一种资源，有可能突破经典情形下t=1的限制，实现多个局部恢复集同时对同一组擦除可用。

Method: 首先形式化定义纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码与可用性（EA-qLRC with availability），建立Singleton型上界；然后利用经典线性码的范德蒙德奇偶校验矩阵给出随机构造；最后通过将多个经典码族（Tamo-Barg码、纤维积码、代数几何码如单点Hermitian和Suzuki码）及其折叠版本转化为纠缠辅助量子码，提供显式构造。

Result: 证明了共享纠缠能够突破经典约束，使得t>1可行（多个局部恢复集可供同一组擦除使用）；建立了Singleton型界限以保证码参数的最优性；从多个经典码族及其折叠版本获得了显式构造，验证了理论框架的普适性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文为纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码引入了可用性概念，在理论和构造层面均取得了进展：既证明了共享纠缠在恢复多样性上的优势，又提供了系统性的构造方法，为量子存储中的容错与数据恢复提供了新的编码方案。

Abstract: In this work, we define entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes with availability, in which any set of up to $δ-1$ erased qudits can be recovered from any one of $t$ local recovery sets, each of size at most $r+δ-1$, with the recovery sets intersecting exactly in the erased coordinates, where $r$ is a (small) positive integer. We show that shared entanglement permits $t>1$, meaning that multiple local recovery sets can be available for the same set of up to $δ-1$ erasures. We establish a Singleton-like bound for this family of codes and present random constructions based on classical linear codes with Vandermonde parity-check matrices. We also provide explicit constructions of entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes with availability from several classical code families and their folded versions, including Tamo-Barg codes, fiber-product codes, and algebraic-geometry codes such as one-point Hermitian and Suzuki codes.

</details>

### [55] [ScaleSense: Cost-Intelligent Scaling Framework via Learned Resource Estimation in Alibaba AnalyticDB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07945)
*Yifan Wu,Yuhan Li,Zhenhua Wang,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou,Zonghao Chen,Liang Lin,Huan Li,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ScaleSense是一个面向云原生无服务器数据仓库的查询级主动性资源缩放框架，通过多面查询编码器、分位数资源预测器和自动缩放控制器来解决资源过度配置问题，在136万条生产查询上实现了76.7%的最优资源配置改善，并将成本降低最高5.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生无服务器数据仓库通过存储与计算解耦实现细粒度弹性，但为高度异构的即席查询确定最优资源分配仍是一项严峻的工业挑战。对阿里云AnalyticDB生产工作负载的分析揭示了代价高昂的"配置陷阱"：用户因恐惧灾难性资源耗尽而盲目过度配置资源，浪费巨额资金，且无法缓解非CPU瓶颈（如I/O饱和）。

Method: ScaleSense包含三个关键组件：(1) 多面查询编码器，联合建模查询计划拓扑和硬件规格；(2) 基于分位数的资源预测器，估计多维度物理资源足迹，作为最优资源缩放的安全网；(3) 自动缩放控制器，在性能-成本帕累托前沿上进行导航，动态调整资源分配以适应特定业务优先级，且无需模型重训练。

Result: 在超过136万条生产查询上的评估显示：ScaleSense在预测精度和预测区间覆盖率上达到最先进水平；相对于最优基线，在最优资源配置选择上实现76.7%的相对改进；性能优化策略下满足用户定义的性能要求同时将资金成本降低最高5.22倍；推理延迟开销低，适用于实际生产部署。

Conclusion: ScaleSense有效解决了云原生无服务器数据仓库中的性能-成本权衡问题，通过查询级主动资源缩放打破了"配置陷阱"，证实了其在实际生产环境中的实用价值。

Abstract: Cloud-native serverless data warehouses achieve fine-grained elasticity by decoupling storage from compute, yet determining the optimal resource allocation for highly heterogeneous ad-hoc queries remains a formidable industrial challenge. Our analysis of production workloads in Alibaba AnalyticDB exposes a costly ``provisioning trap'': the fear of catastrophic resource depletion drives users to blindly over-provision resources, wasting immense monetary budgets without alleviating non-CPU bottlenecks (e.g., I/O saturation). To break this impasse, we propose ScaleSense, a proactive, query-level resource scaling framework. Specifically, it features a multi-faceted query encoder that jointly models plan topologies and hardware specifications. Crucially, a quantile-based resource predictor estimates multi-dimensional physical footprints, acting as a reliable safety net for optimal resource scaling. An auto-scaling controller then navigates the performance-cost Pareto frontier, dynamically tailoring allocations to specific business priorities without requiring model retraining. Evaluations on over 1.36 million production queries show that ScaleSense achieves state-of-the-art prediction accuracy with good prediction interval coverage. By achieving a 76.7% relative improvement in optimal resource configuration selection over the best baseline, this approach addresses the critical performance-cost trade-off while maintaining low-overhead inference latency, confirming its practical performance in production deployments. Under the performance-optimization policy, ScaleSense satisfies user-defined performance requirements while reducing monetary cost by up to 5.22x.

</details>


### [56] [Metadata Reconstruction from Values Alone: Recovering Column Semantics in Undocumented Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07946)
*Mike Helwig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出Rosetta系统，将语言模型置于确定性验证框架中，用于从数据本身自动恢复数据库列的含义。通过结构性证据（值指纹、模式库、校验和）约束模型生成语义，该系统在680个配对列上达到0.475的准确率（42%覆盖率），远优于直接用模型（0.223准确率，94%覆盖率），并能在临床数据上解码95.5%的ICD-9代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境数据库中的列名通常是晦涩的标识符，与学术基准中列名清晰的情况截然不同。现有Text-to-SQL系统依赖列名语义，但真实仓库缺乏文档。因此需要从数据本身恢复列和值的含义，这是解决生产环境文本转SQL问题的前置条件。

Method: Rosetta采用两方面协同的架构：(1) 确定性分析器（profiler）提取结构性证据，包括值指纹、26种模式库和校验和判定；(2) 语言模型基于这些证据提出语义假设。每个输出都带有溯源和由其证据类别约束的置信度。系统还采用代码强制提交门（code-enforced commit gate）控制覆盖率，预测先注册再输出。

Result: 在11个BIRD数据库的680个配对列上（标识符被破坏），Rosetta在42%的提交列上达到0.475准确率，而同一模型直接用为0.223（94%覆盖）。在283个双方都输出的列上两者质量持平——说明收益来自选择性而非能力增强。在i2b2临床数据上，解码95.5%的真实ICD-9代码并全部拒答NDC药物代码。查询场景下，在完全模式不透明时门控系统在59%覆盖率下达到86%执行准确率，而朴素翻译器从0.92跌至0.42。

Conclusion: 确定性证据层扮演的是"胜任力检测器"而非"胜任力放大器"的角色——其核心价值在于决定何时说话（覆盖率），而非提升说话质量。该结论在不同骨干模型上可复现，但prompt式弃权策略不可迁移，代码强制的提交门在所有骨干上均有效。作者坦诚报告了负面结果，包括其权威阶梯并非头条效果背后的机制。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL benchmarks ship schemas whose column names already say what the columns mean. Production warehouses are the inverse: cryptic identifiers, partial or absent documentation. We address the problem they pose first: recovering what columns and values mean from the data itself.
  Rosetta places a language model inside a verification harness: a deterministic profiler extracts structural evidence (value fingerprints, a 26-pattern library, checksum verdicts), the model proposes semantics conditioned on that evidence, and every fact carries provenance and a confidence bounded by its evidence class.
  Against human documentation on 680 paired columns across eleven BIRD databases, identifiers destroyed, the harness delivers metadata that is 0.475 accurate on the 42% of columns it commits to, against 0.223 on 94% for the same model used directly. Restricted to the 283 columns where both arms speak, the harness writes no better prose than the model alone; the gain is selection: deterministic evidence governs whether the system speaks (coverage +0.257 [0.128, 0.378]), not how well. The deterministic layer is a competence detector, not a competence amplifier. A backbone swap bounds the claim: the prose finding reproduces, but prompt-requested abstention does not transfer; a code-enforced commit gate (predictions registered first; measured on a third backbone and held-out databases) makes no-evidence coverage 0.000 on every backbone. On a blind i2b2 clinical warehouse Rosetta decodes 95.5% of 134 real ICD-9 codes from values alone and abstains on all 44 NDC drug codes. The catalog supports calibrated abstention at query time: under full schema opacity a naive translator falls from 0.92 to 0.42 execution accuracy while our gate answers at 86% accuracy over 59% coverage. Negative results are reported plainly, including that our own authority ladder is not the mechanism behind the headline.

</details>


### [57] [Scout: Scalable Document Extraction via Data Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08261)
*Yiming Lin,Chiyu Hao,Shreya Shankar,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Scout是一个从大规模文档集合中提取数据的工具，通过生成和精炼"规则"（程序），仅需读取文档中的少量相关片段而非整个文档，在保持与前沿LLM相当准确率的同时，成本降低61倍到1000倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM能够准确地从大型文档集合中提取数据，但处理整个集合的成本过高。真实世界的文档集合具有丰富的相似性，同一查询在相似文档中的答案往往出现在相似位置，因此LLM只需读取小范围内容而非整个文档。然而先前利用这种相似性的方法存在不足：要么假设文档具有刚性结构，要么假设答案是输入的子串集合并依赖LLM生成程序直接返回，但即使是前沿智能体也难以生成有效的定位程序，因为搜索空间大且从小样本学到的程序容易过拟合。

Method: Scout的方法包括：(1) 从少量采样文档中生成广泛的规则集；(2) 通过选择帕累托最优子集来精炼规则，在低成本下不牺牲准确性（证明规则精炼是NP-hard问题，给出具有可证明近似保证的贪心解法）；(3) 针对仅部分相似的文档集合（相似性存在于文档簇内），使用无需LLM的采样策略从每个簇中采样，以及级联策略选择精炼规则子集，当所选规则不包含答案时回退到未精炼的规则集。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，Scout达到了最强的基线（读取每个完整文档的前沿LLM智能体）的准确率，同时在1,000篇文档的集合上成本降低了61倍到1000倍以上，并且比最强的前基于程序的方案准确率高61%。

Conclusion: Scout成功地展示了通过程序化规则利用文档间相似性可以在保持高准确率的同时大幅降低LLM提取数据的成本，并且通过理论分析（NP-hard证明和近似保证）和级联策略设计，能够稳健地处理部分相似的文档集合。

Abstract: Extracting values from large document collections powers data analysis across many domains. Frontier LLMs extract such values accurately, but processing an
  entire collection with one is prohibitively costly. Yet this cost is largely avoidable: real-world collections exhibit rich similarity, so for the same query
  over similar documents, the answer tends to recur in similar locations; an LLM need only read that small span, not the whole document. Prior methods that
  exploit this similarity fall short: they either assume a rigid document structure, or assume the answer is a set of substrings of the input and use an
  LLM-generated program to return it directly. Even a frontier agent fails to generate effective programs to directly locate the answer's span, as the search
  space is large and programs learned from a small sample tend to overfit. We present Scout, a tool that generates accurate and cost-effective programs (that
  we call rules) to extract data at scale. From a few sampled documents, Scout generates a broad rule set and refines it by selecting a pareto-optimal subset
  with low cost without sacrificing accuracy. We prove rule refinement is NP-hard and give a greedy solution with a provable approximation guarantee. Scout
  handles collections that are only partly similar, where similarity holds within clusters of documents. In this setting, a sampling strategy, using no LLM,
  draws samples from each cluster; and a cascade strategy selects a subset of refined rules, falling back to the unrefined rule set when the selected rules
  don't contain the answer. Experiments on six real-world datasets show that Scout matches the accuracy of the strongest baseline, a frontier LLM agent that
  reads each full document, while being 61x to over 1000x cheaper on a collection of 1,000 documents, and is 61% more accurate than the strongest prior
  program-based approach.

</details>


### [58] [InSituANN: Revisiting IVF for PCIe-Efficient Billion-Scale Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08751)
*Yuemeng Xu,Zongxi Liu,Junyu Long,Yiming Huang,Jiarui Guo,Yangyujia Wang,Jiachen Xu,Dongyuan Yu,Zongwei Lv,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: InSituANN 是一个基于 IVF 的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）引擎，在单个商用 GPU 上实现了十亿级向量检索。它将原始基础向量保存在主机内存中，在 GPU 上执行紧凑路由和可选剪枝，同时在主机端进行原位（in-situ）精细搜索，从而避免了通过 PCIe 传输高维基础向量的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模向量检索已成为现代检索系统的核心算子，但 GPU 的 VRAM 容量有限，难以在单 GPU 上部署十亿级索引。现有的 CPU-GPU 异构方案虽然将基础向量放在主机内存中，但将精细搜索卸载到 GPU 会导致大量基础向量数据需要通过 PCIe 流式传输，成为新的性能瓶颈。

Method: InSituANN 采用 IVF 索引结构，将原始基础向量保留在主机内存中，在主机端原位执行精细搜索；GPU 仅用于紧凑路由（compact routing）和可选剪枝（pruning）。此外，论文还设计了超快速的 IVF 索引构建路径，大幅缩短索引构建时间。

Result: 在 SIFT-1B 上，InSituANN 仅用 5.2 分钟构建 IVF 索引，比 HNSW 的 30.4 小时构建速度快约 350 倍。在十亿级数据集上达到相同召回率时，端到端吞吐量比 PCIe 受限的 Rummy 基线提升 104.9x-4298.2x，比 DiskANN 在 SIFT-1B 和 DEEP-1B 上提升 2.4x-4.6x。

Conclusion: InSituANN 通过避免 PCIe 传输高维基础向量的巧妙设计，在保持 IVF 简单性的同时，实现了在成本友好的单 GPU 硬件上进行十亿级向量检索的实际可行性，并提供了优于图基方法的召回-吞吐量权衡和更低的索引空间占用。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) over billion-scale vector datasets has become a foundational operator for modern retrieval systems, powering large-scale recommendation, semantic search, and LLM/RAG workloads. Although GPUs offer massive parallelism and high-bandwidth memory for batched vector search, their limited VRAM capacity makes fully GPU-resident billion-scale indexes difficult to deploy. In CPU-GPU heterogeneous designs, keeping the base vectors in host memory avoids this capacity limit, but naively offloading fine search to the GPU introduces a new bottleneck: large volumes of base-vector data must be streamed over PCIe.
  We present InSituANN, an IVF-based ANNS engine that enables billion-scale vector search on a single commodity GPU. InSituANN keeps original base vectors in host memory, performs fine search in situ, and uses the GPU for compact routing and optional pruning. As a result, query processing avoids PCIe transfers of high-dimensional base vectors while retaining the simplicity of IVF. Beyond query performance, we further design an ultra-fast IVF construction path for InSituANN. On SIFT-1B, InSituANN builds the IVF index in 5.2 minutes, about 350x faster than the measured 30.4-hour HNSW build. At matched recall on billion-scale datasets, InSituANN improves end-to-end throughput by 104.9x-4298.2x over the PCIe-bound Rummy baseline and by 2.4x-4.6x over DiskANN on SIFT-1B and DEEP-1B. Together with strong recall-throughput trade-offs and lower index space than graph-based alternatives, these gains make billion-scale retrieval practical on cost-efficient hardware.
  We open-source InSituANN at https://github.com/mindtravel/InSituANN-OpenSource.

</details>


### [59] [SiriusDeliver: Automating Data Warehouse Delivery at Tencent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09185)
*Haining Xie,Xiaokai Zhou,Jiaming Yang,Siqi Shen,Ziwei Wang,Yifeng Zheng,Tengyue Xu,Yipeng Shi,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen,Jie Jiang,Debiao He,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出SiriusDeliver，一个用于生产数据仓库任务交付的端到端自动化代理系统。该系统通过层次化交付代理、工件生命周期控制模块和轨迹驱动的技能进化机制三个组件，实现了从上下文检索到失败诊断的全流程自动化。在腾讯云WeData的大规模生产部署中，服务了3,600名月活用户和18,240次交付会话，实现了87.2%的端到端成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据仓库（DW）任务交付是一个复杂的生产过程，涉及上下文检索、工作流配置、代码生成、平台提交和失败诊断等多个环节。虽然LLM和编码代理改善了软件开发，但不足以应对生产级DW交付的需求，因为后者需要依赖感知的编排、生命周期感知的工件控制以及对平台实践演进的持续适应。

Method: SiriusDeliver包含三个核心组件：(1) 层次化交付代理，用于编排仓库技能；(2) 工件生命周期控制模块，在平台执行前后验证和修正工件；(3) 轨迹驱动的技能进化机制，从交付轨迹中维护可复用的技能。

Result: 离线实验显示SiriusDeliver在交付成功率和自动化效率上优于代表性基线。在跨6个业务团队和4种仓库任务类型的两月部署中，服务了3,600名月活用户和18,240次交付会话，实现87.2%端到端成功率和73.5%自主提交率。A/B测试显示将中位交付时间从228分钟降至23分钟，工程师工作量从95分钟降至11分钟。

Conclusion: SiriusDeliver证明了LLM代理能够在生产环境中实现数据仓库任务交付的高度自动化，大幅降低交付时间和人工成本，同时保持相当的最终交付成功率。该系统通过技能进化和生命周期控制的有机结合，有效解决了生产DW交付的编排、工件控制和平台适应等关键挑战。

Abstract: Enterprise data warehouses (DWs) support business-critical analytics, but warehouse task delivery remains a complicated production process involving context retrieval, workflow configuration, code generation, platform submission, and failure diagnosis. Although large language models (LLMs) and coding agents have improved software development, they are insufficient for production DW delivery, which requires dependency-aware orchestration, lifecycle-aware artifact control, and continuous adaptation to evolving platform practices. We present SiriusDeliver, an end-to-end delivery automation agent for production warehouse task submission. SiriusDeliver integrates three components: a hierarchical delivery agent that orchestrates warehouse skills, an artifact lifecycle control module that verifies and revises artifacts before and after platform execution, and a trace-driven skill evolution mechanism that maintains reusable skills from delivery trajectories. We evaluate SiriusDeliver through offline datasets and large-scale production deployment on Tencent Cloud WeData. Offline experiments on real-world warehouse delivery cases show that SiriusDeliver improves delivery success and automation efficiency over representative baselines. During a two-month deployment across 6 business teams and 4 warehouse task types, SiriusDeliver served 3,600 monthly active users and supported 18,240 delivery sessions, achieving an 87.2% end-to-end success rate and a 73.5% autonomous submission rate. A one-month A/B test shows that SiriusDeliver reduces median delivery time from 228 to 23 minutes and engineer effort from 95 to 11 minutes, while maintaining comparable final delivery success.

</details>


### [60] [AkasicDB: Demonstrating Omni RAG with a Unified Vector-Graph-Relational DBMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09214)
*Geonho Lee,Jeongho Park,Donghyoung Han,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出AkasicDB，一个原生支持向量-图-关系联合检索的统一数据库系统，实现首个原生集成的Omni RAG工作流，通过交互式演示展示其相对纯向量检索的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统结合向量检索与结构化知识（如Graph RAG、Filtered vector search），但数据库架构依赖外部管道或非原生集成，导致高开销，难以高效支持复杂RAG工作流。

Method: AkasicDB在统一执行框架内联合执行向量相似度搜索、图遍历和关系过滤，扩展了先前工作Chimera，增加原生向量支持，实现Vector-Graph-Relational三种检索的统一执行（即Omni RAG）。

Result: 通过交互式聊天演示，用户可执行并可视化Omni RAG查询，体验其在检索和推理上优于纯向量方法，同时观察到现有数据库架构在支持Omni RAG时的实际局限性。

Conclusion: AkasicDB展示了数据库系统原生集成多模态RAG工作流的可行性，为复杂检索增强生成提供了更高效的架构方向。

Abstract: Recent Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly combine vector retrieval with structured knowledge, such as Graph RAG and Filtered vector search. However, existing database architectures struggle to support such complex RAG workflows efficiently, as they rely on out-of-DB pipelines or in-DB non-native integration, leading to high overhead. This demo paper presents AkasicDB, a database system that natively supports such RAG workflows by jointly executing vector similarity search, graph traversal, and relational filtering within a single execution framework. AkasicDB extends our prior work, Chimera, with native vector support to enable such unified execution. Based on AkasicDB, we demonstrate the first native integration of Vector-Graph-Relational RAG, which we refer to as Omni RAG. Through an interactive chat-style demonstration, users execute and visualize Omni RAG queries, directly experiencing its superior retrieval and reasoning over vector-only approaches while observing the practical limitations of existing database architectures in supporting Omni RAG. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/8d09_dtrEIM

</details>


### [61] [SafeQL: Search-based Refinement for Safe and Efficient LLM-based Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09260)
*Geonho Lee,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SafeQL提出了一种基于搜索的SQL查询细化范式，将DBMS从被动角色转变为主动指导者。与失败后重新生成整个查询不同，SafeQL通过解释DBMS反馈来增量修复错误组件，在"安全查询空间"内进行引导式搜索，并通过DBMS执行逐步验证候选查询，最终收敛到可执行的SQL。在Bird和Spider基准上的实验表明，该方法比基于重新生成的方案显著提升了执行准确率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的Text-to-SQL系统虽然无需微调即可工作，但生成的SQL查询常常因引用不存在的表、属性、函数或值而在数据库架构下无效。现有方法在查询失败时仅依赖DBMS的错误信息进行重新生成，DBMS在查询细化过程中处于被动角色，导致重复生成相同错误，效率低下。

Method: SafeQL提出一种搜索式细化范式：(1) 将DBMS反馈解读为对错误组件的定位信息，而非仅仅作为错误消息；(2) 仅增量修复错误的查询组件，而非重新生成整个查询；(3) 定义"安全查询空间"，将细化步骤建模为在该空间内的引导式搜索；(4) 通过DBMS实际执行逐步验证候选查询，确保收敛到可执行查询。

Result: 在Bird和Spider两个标准基准数据集上的实验表明，SafeQL相比基于重新生成的方法，显著提升了执行准确率（execution accuracy）和执行效率（efficiency）。

Conclusion: SafeQL证明通过重新定义DBMS在查询细化过程中的角色（从被动接收者变为主动引导者），并采用增量修复而非整体重生成的策略，可以有效解决LLM生成SQL查询时持续出错的问题，为Text-to-SQL系统的可靠性提升提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced Text-to-SQL by enabling natural language interfaces to databases without task-specific fine-tuning. However, existing LLM-based systems remain unreliable, often generating SQL queries that are invalid under the database schema, referencing non-existent tables, attributes, functions, or values. Such errors persist because interactions with the database management system (DBMS) are typically limited to error messages, leaving it in a largely passive role during query refinement. This paper proposes SafeQL, \textit{a search-based refinement paradigm that redefines the role of the DBMS as an active guide in the refinement process}. Instead of regenerating entire queries after execution failure, SafeQL interprets DBMS feedback to incrementally repair only the erroneous components. Each refinement step is formulated as a guided search within a \textit{safe query space}, where candidate queries are progressively validated through DBMS execution, thereby converging to an executable query and preventing repeated regeneration of errors. Experiments on the Bird and Spider benchmarks show that SafeQL significantly improves execution accuracy and efficiency compared to regeneration-based methods.

</details>


### [62] ["Will This Data Break My Task?" - Interactive Synthesis of Task-Aware Data Unit Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09376)
*Hao Chen,Arnab Phani,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PrismaDV是一个复合AI系统，通过联合分析数据与下游任务代码，合成任务感知的数据单元测试，并维护数据-代码假设图来追踪数据约束与源代码之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据验证框架仅从观测数据本身推导数据单元测试，忽略了消耗数据的下游代码语义，导致测试无法捕获与任务相关的隐含数据假设。

Method: PrismaDV将测试生成分解为多个LLM驱动的步骤：数据画像(profiling)、列访问检测、任务代码中的数据流分析、隐含数据假设推断，随后合成数据单元测试代码，并维护一个内部"数据-代码假设图"将生成的数据约束链接回任务源代码。系统还包含自定义提示优化器以适配特定数据集。

Result: 通过交互式Web界面在5个真实世界数据集和60个下游任务上演示，支持自然语言假设与可执行数据约束的合成、检查与精炼，可将任务感知测试与任务无关基线在错误数据批次上进行对比。

Conclusion: PrismaDV通过结合数据与下游代码语义，生成比任务无关基线更精准的任务感知数据单元测试，提高了数据管道中数据验证的可靠性。

Abstract: Data is a central resource for modern enterprises and institutions, and data errors propagating through data pipelines lead to serious impact in production. Therefore, data validation is essential for ensuring the reliability of downstream applications. This led to the development of data unit tests, executable programs that test data before moving it around through large data pipelines. However, existing frameworks derive data unit tests from observed data alone, ignoring the semantics of the code that consumes the data downstream.
  To this end, we present PrismaDV, a compound AI system that synthesizes task-aware data unit tests for tabular data by jointly analyzing data and downstream task code. PrismaDV decomposes the test generation into multiple LLM-powered steps: data profiling, detection of column accesses, data flow analysis in the task code, and the inference of implicit data assumptions. It subsequently synthesizes code for the data unit test, and maintains an internal ``data-code assumption graph'' that links generated data constraints back to the task's source code.
  We demonstrate PrismaDV through an interactive web-based interface where attendees run the system on five real-world datasets with 60 downstream tasks, synthesize, inspect and refine both natural language assumptions about the data and executable data constraints. The interface allows attendees to navigate the data-code assumption graph, compare task-aware data unit tests against task-agnostic baselines on erroneous data batches, and interactively edit assumptions and data constraints. Furthermore, attendees can observe how a custom prompt optimizer adapts the system to specific datasets over time.

</details>


### [63] [Monotonicity-Guided Bottom-Up Petri Net Discovery: The SPECpp Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09398)
*Leah Tacke genannt Unterberg,Lisa L. Mannel,Wil M. P. van der Aalst*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPECpp框架，一种基于Petri网的自底向上过程发现方法。与Inductive Miner等自顶向下方法不同，SPECpp利用Petri网局部位置的单调性质，使序列、选择、循环和并发等结构自然涌现，而非依赖预定义构造。该框架通过多种策略解决候选位置及其组合指数级增长的核心挑战，在时间和资源约束下获取高质量模型，并支持快速实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程发现是过程挖掘的核心挑战之一。现有自顶向下方法（如Inductive Miner）依赖预定义的构造模式，限制了模型表达力。Petri网具有强大的表达能力（包括自由选择构造和长期依赖），但其全局行为难以分析。作者希望利用Petri网局部位置的单调性质实现自底向上的发现，使复杂结构自然涌现，充分发挥Petri网的全部表达能力。

Method: 提出SPECpp框架，采用自底向上的Petri网发现方法。核心思想是利用个体位置的单调属性来高效表征过程行为，从而避免繁琐的全局分析。针对候选位置及组合呈指数级增长的主要挑战，SPECpp实现了多种搜索与优化策略，在时间和资源约束下获取高质量的过程模型，并提供快速实验环境以评估不同策略。

Result: 使用合成数据和真实事件日志对SPECpp框架中的多种策略进行了系统评估，验证了其在高资源约束下发现高质量Petri网模型的有效性，能够表达包括自由选择构造和长期依赖在内的复杂行为。

Conclusion: SPECpp展示了自底向上Petri网过程发现的可行性，相比依赖预定义结构的自顶向下方法，能更充分地利用Petri网的表达力，同时通过有效策略管理计算复杂度，为过程发现提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Process discovery is one of the central challenges in process mining. Petri nets are particularly attractive because simple local constructs can express complex behavior, including concurrency. While their global behavior may be difficult to analyze, individual places can be efficiently characterized using monotonic properties, enabling bottom-up discovery. Unlike top-down approaches such as the Inductive Miner, which rely on predefined constructs for sequences, choices, loops, and concurrency, our approach allows such structures to emerge organically and can exploit the full expressive power of Petri nets, including free-choice constructs and long-term dependencies. The main challenge is the exponential number of candidate places and their combinations. We present the SPECpp framework which implements strategies to obtain high-quality models under time and resource constraints. SPECpp supports rapid experimentation and is used to evaluate these strategies using both synthetic and real-life event data.

</details>


### [64] [Carnot: Interpretable, Interactive, and Optimized Execution of Deep Research Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09532)
*Matthew Russo,Yash Agarwal,Tianyu Li,Zhuohan Gu,Michael Cafarella,Omar Khattab,Tim Kraska,Samuel Madden*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Carnot是一个面向AI驱动数据分析的交互式执行引擎，将自然语言请求编译为物理执行图，并通过交互式笔记本界面呈现，使用户能够审查计划、逐步执行算子、检查中间数据、编辑代码或语义算子指令，同时支持基于成本或延迟约束的查询优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业数据湖自然语言查询方案存在两难：深度研究代理（deep research agents）是黑盒，隐藏中间推理和数据检索步骤，缺乏对API成本和延迟的控制；而语义算子方法在企业规模数据湖上成本过高。分析人员缺乏拦截幻觉前提、验证中间结果或纠正系统轨迹的能力。

Method: Carnot将自然语言请求编译成物理执行图，通过交互式笔记本界面展示。用户可批判性审查计划、逐步执行算子、检查中间数据，或直接编辑底层代码和语义算子指令。系统内置查询优化器，根据用户提供的成本或延迟约束进行优化。

Result: 论文展示了Carnot如何帮助用户在基于真实企业用例的负载上实现高效且可验证的分析洞察（演示形式呈现）。

Conclusion: Carnot通过将黑盒AI代理转变为透明、可控的交互式执行流程，赋予用户在数据湖上进行AI驱动分析时的可验证性与可干预性，同时兼顾成本和延迟的优化。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly seek to query data lakes using natural language via AI-driven tools like semantic operators or deep research agents. However, the latter operates as an opaque black box, hiding its intermediate reasoning and data retrieval steps, and failing to expose controls for managing API costs and execution latency. Meanwhile, the former can be prohibitively expensive for enterprise-scale data lakes. Consequently, analysts using these systems lack the agency to intercept hallucinated premises, verify intermediate results, or correct the system's trajectory. We present Carnot, an interactive execution engine for AI-driven analytics. Carnot compiles natural language requests into physical execution graphs and surfaces them through an interactive notebook interface. Rather than waiting blindly for a final output, users can critique the plan, incrementally execute operators, inspect intermediate data, or directly edit the underlying code or semantic operator instructions. Carnot's query optimizer will optimize the query with respect to cost or latency constraints provided by the user. Our demo will showcase how Carnot helps users achieve efficient and verifiable insights on workloads motivated by real enterprise use cases.

</details>
